# 17. BINANCE CLIENT
############################
     1	import hashlib, hmac, time
     2	from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_DOWN
     3	from urllib.parse import urlencode
     4	import requests
     5	
     6	class BinanceAPIError(RuntimeError): pass
     7	
     8	class BinanceSpotClient:
     9	    def __init__(self, api_key, api_secret, testnet=True, recv_window=5000, timeout=20):
    10	        self.api_key=api_key; self.api_secret=api_secret; self.testnet=bool(testnet)
    11	        self.base_url='https://testnet.binance.vision' if self.testnet else 'https://api.binance.com'
    12	        self.recv_window=int(recv_window); self.timeout=timeout
    13	        self.session=requests.Session(); self.session.headers.update({'X-MBX-APIKEY': self.api_key})
    14	        self.time_offset_ms=0
    15	    def _request(self, method, path, params=None, signed=False):
    16	        """
    17	        Execute a Binance REST request.
    18	
    19	        GET requests are safe to retry on transient network failures.
    20	        POST/DELETE requests are intentionally NOT retried because repeating
    21	        an order/cancel request can create dangerous duplicate side effects.
    22	
    23	        Signed requests rebuild timestamp/signature on every attempt so a
    24	        retry cannot reuse an expired timestamp.
    25	        """
    26	        method = method.upper()
    27	        max_attempts = 3 if method == "GET" else 1
    28	        delays = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
    29	
    30	        last_exc = None
    31	
    32	        for attempt in range(max_attempts):
    33	            p = dict(params or {})
    34	
    35	            if signed:
    36	                p.setdefault('recvWindow', self.recv_window)
    37	                p['timestamp'] = int(time.time() * 1000) + self.time_offset_ms
    38	                query = urlencode(p, doseq=True)
    39	                p['signature'] = hmac.new(
    40	                    self.api_secret.encode(),
    41	                    query.encode(),
    42	                    hashlib.sha256,
    43	                ).hexdigest()
    44	
    45	            try:
    46	                r = self.session.request(
    47	                    method,
    48	                    self.base_url + path,
    49	                    params=p,
    50	                    timeout=self.timeout,
    51	                )
    52	            except (requests.Timeout, requests.ConnectionError) as exc:
    53	                last_exc = exc
    54	
    55	                if attempt + 1 >= max_attempts:
    56	                    raise
    57	
    58	                time.sleep(delays[attempt])
    59	
    60	                continue
    61	
    62	            try:
    63	                payload = r.json()
    64	            except ValueError:
    65	                payload = {'code': r.status_code, 'msg': r.text}
    66	
    67	            if r.status_code >= 400 or (
    68	                isinstance(payload, dict)
    69	                and payload.get('code', 0) < 0
    70	            ):
    71	                raise BinanceAPIError(
    72	                    f'Binance {r.status_code}: {payload}'
    73	                )
    74	
    75	            return payload
    76	
    77	        if last_exc is not None:
    78	            raise last_exc
    79	
    80	        raise BinanceAPIError(
    81	            f'Binance request failed: {method} {path}'
    82	        )
    83	    def sync_time(self):
    84	        server=self._request('GET','/api/v3/time'); self.time_offset_ms=int(server['serverTime'])-int(time.time()*1000); return server
    85	    def ping(self): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/ping')
    86	    def exchange_info(self,symbol=None): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/exchangeInfo',{'symbol':symbol} if symbol else {})
    87	    def ticker_price(self,symbol): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/ticker/price',{'symbol':symbol})
    88	    def book_ticker(self,symbol): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/ticker/bookTicker',{'symbol':symbol})
    89	    def klines(self,symbol,interval,limit=200): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/klines',{'symbol':symbol,'interval':interval,'limit':limit})
    90	    def account(self): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/account',signed=True)
    91	    def all_orders(self,symbol,limit=1000): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/allOrders',{'symbol':symbol,'limit':limit},signed=True)
    92	    def order_list(self,symbol,order_list_id=None,list_client_order_id=None):
    93	        p={'symbol':symbol}
    94	        if order_list_id is not None: p['orderListId']=order_list_id
    95	        if list_client_order_id is not None: p['origClientOrderId']=list_client_order_id
    96	        return self._request('GET','/api/v3/orderList',p,signed=True)
    97	    def open_order_lists(self,symbol=None): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/openOrderList',{},signed=True)
    98	    def all_order_lists(self,symbol=None,limit=100): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/allOrderList',{'limit':limit},signed=True)
    99	    def open_orders(self,symbol=None): return self._request('GET','/api/v3/openOrders',{'symbol':symbol} if symbol else {},signed=True)
   100	    def websocket_signature_params(self, recv_window=None):
   101	        params = {
   102	            "apiKey": self.api_key,
   103	            "timestamp": int(time.time() * 1000) + self.time_offset_ms,
   104	            "recvWindow": int(
   105	                recv_window if recv_window is not None else self.recv_window
   106	            ),
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        # Binance signed requests use the canonical query-string form.
   110	        query = urlencode(sorted(params.items()), doseq=True)
   111	
   112	        params["signature"] = hmac.new(
   113	            self.api_secret.encode(),
   114	            query.encode(),
   115	            hashlib.sha256,
   116	        ).hexdigest()
   117	
   118	        return params
   119	
   120	    def create_user_listen_token(self):
   121	        raise BinanceAPIError(
   122	            'Spot Testnet does not support /sapi/v1/userListenToken; '
   123	            'use userDataStream.subscribe.signature'
   124	        )
   125	    def order(self,symbol,side,type_,quantity=None,quote_order_qty=None,price=None,stop_price=None,time_in_force=None,new_client_order_id=None):
   126	        p={'symbol':symbol,'side':side,'type':type_,'newOrderRespType':'FULL'}
   127	        if quantity is not None:p['quantity']=quantity
   128	        if quote_order_qty is not None:p['quoteOrderQty']=quote_order_qty
   129	        if price is not None:p['price']=price
   130	        if stop_price is not None:p['stopPrice']=stop_price
   131	        if time_in_force is not None:p['timeInForce']=time_in_force
   132	        if new_client_order_id:p['newClientOrderId']=new_client_order_id
   133	        return self._request('POST','/api/v3/order',p,signed=True)
   134	    def get_order(self,symbol,order_id=None,orig_client_order_id=None):
   135	        p={'symbol':symbol}
   136	        if order_id is not None:p['orderId']=order_id
   137	        if orig_client_order_id is not None:p['origClientOrderId']=orig_client_order_id
   138	        return self._request('GET','/api/v3/order',p,signed=True)
   139	    def cancel_order(self,symbol,order_id=None,orig_client_order_id=None):
   140	        p={'symbol':symbol}
   141	        if order_id is not None:p['orderId']=order_id
   142	        if orig_client_order_id is not None:p['origClientOrderId']=orig_client_order_id
   143	        return self._request('DELETE','/api/v3/order',p,signed=True)
   144	    def cancel_open_orders(self,symbol): return self._request('DELETE','/api/v3/openOrders',{'symbol':symbol},signed=True)
   145	    def create_oco_sell(self,symbol,quantity,take_profit_price,stop_price,stop_limit_price,list_client_order_id=None):
   146	        p={'symbol':symbol,'side':'SELL','quantity':quantity,'aboveType':'TAKE_PROFIT_LIMIT','abovePrice':take_profit_price,'aboveStopPrice':take_profit_price,'aboveTimeInForce':'GTC','belowType':'STOP_LOSS_LIMIT','belowStopPrice':stop_price,'belowPrice':stop_limit_price,'belowTimeInForce':'GTC','newOrderRespType':'FULL'}
   147	        if list_client_order_id:p['listClientOrderId']=list_client_order_id
   148	        return self._request('POST','/api/v3/orderList/oco',p,signed=True)
   149	    def cancel_oco(self,symbol,order_list_id=None,list_client_order_id=None):
   150	        p={'symbol':symbol}
   151	        if order_list_id is not None:p['orderListId']=order_list_id
   152	        if list_client_order_id is not None:p['listClientOrderId']=list_client_order_id
   153	        return self._request('DELETE','/api/v3/orderList',p,signed=True)
   154	    @staticmethod
   155	    def decimal_floor(value,step): return (Decimal(str(value))/Decimal(str(step))).to_integral_value(rounding=ROUND_DOWN)*Decimal(str(step))
   156	    @staticmethod
   157	    def decimal_format(value): return format(Decimal(str(value)).normalize(),'f')

# 18. MARKET SCANNER
############################
     1	import os
     2	import logging
     3	from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
     4	from typing import List, Optional
     5	
     6	from data import fetch_klines
     7	from strategy import calculate_indicators, config_from_env
     8	
     9	
    10	log = logging.getLogger("williams-scanner")
    11	
    12	
    13	@dataclass
    14	class Candidate:
    15	    symbol: str
    16	    score: float
    17	    signal: bool
    18	
    19	    # Strategy/setup information
    20	    setup_score: float
    21	    signal_strength: float
    22	    breakout_distance_pct: float
    23	
    24	    # Risk / market information
    25	    risk_pct: float
    26	    risk_reward: float
    27	    atr_pct: float
    28	    spread_pct: float
    29	
    30	    htf_confirmed: bool
    31	
    32	    # Human-readable state
    33	    setup_state: str
    34	    reason: str = ""
    35	
    36	    def to_dict(self):
    37	        return asdict(self)
    38	
    39	
    40	class MarketScanner:
    41	    """
    42	    Finds and ranks long setups across configured USDT symbols.
    43	
    44	    IMPORTANT:
    45	    - This component NEVER places orders.
    46	    - long_signal remains the strict entry condition.
    47	    - SETUP_READY candidates are informational/ranking candidates only.
    48	    """
    49	
    50	    def __init__(self, client, symbols=None, interval=None):
    51	        self.client = client
    52	
    53	        self.interval = interval or os.getenv(
    54	            "INTERVAL",
    55	            "1h",
    56	        )
    57	
    58	        self.symbols = symbols or self._load_symbols()
    59	
    60	        self.atr_period = int(
    61	            os.getenv("ATR_PERIOD", "14")
    62	        )
    63	
    64	        self.max_atr_pct = float(
    65	            os.getenv("MAX_ATR_PCT", "0.08")
    66	        )
    67	
    68	        self.max_spread_pct = float(
    69	            os.getenv("MAX_SPREAD_PCT", "0.0015")
    70	        )
    71	        self.require_htf_confirmation = (
    72	            os.getenv(
    73	                "REQUIRE_HTF_CONFIRMATION",
    74	                "true"
    75	            ).lower() == "true"
    76	        )
    77	        self.min_risk_reward = float(
    78	            os.getenv("MIN_RISK_REWARD", "1.5")
    79	        )
    80	
    81	        self.stop_pct = float(
    82	            os.getenv("STOP_LOSS_PCT", "0.02")
    83	        )
    84	
    85	        self.target_pct = float(
    86	            os.getenv("TAKE_PROFIT_PCT", "0.04")
    87	        )
    88	
    89	        self.min_rr = float(
    90	            os.getenv("MIN_RISK_REWARD", "1.5")
    91	        )
    92	
    93	        self.htf_interval = os.getenv(
    94	            "HTF_INTERVAL",
    95	            "4h",
    96	        )
    97	
    98	    def _load_symbols(self):
    99	        raw = os.getenv(
   100	            "SCAN_SYMBOLS",
   101	            "BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,SOLUSDT,BNBUSDT,XRPUSDT,"
   102	            "ADAUSDT,DOGEUSDT,AVAXUSDT,LINKUSDT,DOTUSDT",
   103	        )
   104	
   105	        return [
   106	            x.strip().upper()
   107	            for x in raw.split(",")
   108	            if x.strip()
   109	        ]
   110	
   111	    @staticmethod
   112	    def _atr(df, period):
   113	        prev = df["close"].shift(1)
   114	
   115	        import pandas as pd
   116	
   117	        tr = pd.concat(
   118	            [
   119	                df["high"] - df["low"],
   120	                (df["high"] - prev).abs(),
   121	                (df["low"] - prev).abs(),
   122	            ],
   123	            axis=1,
   124	        ).max(axis=1)
   125	
   126	        return float(
   127	            tr.rolling(period).mean().iloc[-1]
   128	        )
   129	
   130	    def _spread(self, symbol):
   131	        book = self.client.book_ticker(symbol)
   132	
   133	        bid = float(book["bidPrice"])
   134	        ask = float(book["askPrice"])
   135	
   136	        mid = (bid + ask) / 2
   137	
   138	        if mid <= 0:
   139	            return 1.0
   140	
   141	        return (ask - bid) / mid
   142	
   143	    def _symbol_is_valid(self, symbol):
   144	        info = self.client.exchange_info(symbol)
   145	        symbols = info.get("symbols", [])
   146	
   147	        if len(symbols) != 1:
   148	            return False
   149	
   150	        item = symbols[0]
   151	
   152	        if item.get("status") != "TRADING":
   153	            return False
   154	
   155	        if item.get("quoteAsset") != "USDT":
   156	            return False
   157	
   158	        return True
   159	
   160	    def _htf_confirmation(self, symbol):
   161	        if (
   162	            os.getenv(
   163	                "REQUIRE_HTF_CONFIRMATION",
   164	                "true",
   165	            ).lower()
   166	            != "true"
   167	        ):
   168	            return True
   169	
   170	        htf = fetch_klines(
   171	            self.client,
   172	            symbol,
   173	            self.htf_interval,
   174	            limit=160,
   175	        )
   176	
   177	        if len(htf) < 80:
   178	            return False
   179	
   180	        ind = calculate_indicators(
   181	            htf.iloc[:-1].copy(),
   182	            config_from_env(),
   183	        )
   184	
   185	        last = ind.iloc[-1]
   186	
   187	        return bool(
   188	            last.get("bullish_alligator", False)
   189	        ) and float(
   190	            last.get("ao", 0) or 0
   191	        ) > 0
   192	
   193	    @staticmethod
   194	    def _bool(value):
   195	        return bool(value)
   196	
   197	    def _setup_state(self, last):
   198	        """
   199	        Informational setup classification.
   200	
   201	        STRONG_SIGNAL:
   202	            All strict entry conditions are satisfied.
   203	
   204	        SETUP_READY:
   205	            Directional conditions are satisfied and the market is
   206	            close to/at a breakout setup, but strict entry is not yet
   207	            triggered.
   208	
   209	        WATCHING:
   210	            Some bullish conditions exist, but the setup is incomplete.
   211	
   212	        NONE:
   213	            No meaningful bullish setup.
   214	        """
   215	
   216	        strict_signal = bool(
   217	            last.get("long_signal", False)
   218	        )
   219	
   220	        if strict_signal:
   221	            return "STRONG_SIGNAL"
   222	
   223	        bullish = bool(
   224	            last.get("long_bullish", False)
   225	        )
   226	
   227	        awake = bool(
   228	            last.get("long_awake", False)
   229	        )
   230	
   231	        ao = bool(
   232	            last.get("long_ao_positive", False)
   233	        )
   234	
   235	        ac = bool(
   236	            last.get("long_ac_positive", False)
   237	        )
   238	
   239	        fractal = bool(
   240	            last.get("long_fractal_ready", False)
   241	        )
   242	
   243	        if bullish and awake and ao and ac and fractal:
   244	            return "SETUP_READY"
   245	
   246	        if (
   247	            bullish
   248	            or (ao and ac)
   249	            or (
   250	                bullish
   251	                and awake
   252	            )
   253	        ):
   254	            return "WATCHING"
   255	
   256	        return "NONE"
   257	
   258	    def analyse(self, symbol) -> Optional[Candidate]:
   259	        symbol = symbol.upper()
   260	
   261	        try:
   262	            if not self._symbol_is_valid(symbol):
   263	                return None
   264	
   265	            df = fetch_klines(
   266	                self.client,
   267	                symbol,
   268	                self.interval,
   269	                limit=250,
   270	            )
   271	
   272	            if len(df) < 100:
   273	                return None
   274	
   275	            # Never analyse an unfinished candle.
   276	            closed = df.iloc[:-1].copy()
   277	
   278	            indicators = calculate_indicators(
   279	                closed,
   280	                config_from_env(),
   281	            )
   282	
   283	            last = indicators.iloc[-1]
   284	
   285	            setup_state = self._setup_state(last)
   286	
   287	            # We still need a meaningful directional setup.
   288	            if setup_state == "NONE":
   289	                return None
   290	
   291	            price = float(last["close"])
   292	
   293	            if price <= 0:
   294	                return None
   295	
   296	            atr = self._atr(
   297	                closed,
   298	                self.atr_period,
   299	            )
   300	
   301	            if atr <= 0:
   302	                return None
   303	
   304	            atr_pct = atr / price
   305	
   306	            if atr_pct > self.max_atr_pct:
   307	                return None
   308	
   309	            spread_pct = self._spread(symbol)
   310	
   311	            if spread_pct > self.max_spread_pct:
   312	                return None
   313	
   314	            rr = (
   315	                self.target_pct
   316	                / max(self.stop_pct, 1e-9)
   317	            )
   318	
   319	            if rr < self.min_rr:
   320	                return None
   321	
   322	            htf = self._htf_confirmation(symbol)
   323	
   324	            # HTF confirmation remains required for an actual
   325	            # STRONG_SIGNAL, but SETUP_READY candidates can still
   326	            # be observed/ranked.
   327	            strict_signal = bool(
   328	                last.get("long_signal", False)
   329	            )
   330	
   331	            if strict_signal and not htf:
   332	                return None
   333	
   334	            setup_score = float(
   335	                last.get("long_setup_score", 0.0)
   336	            )
   337	
   338	            breakout_distance_pct = float(
   339	                last.get(
   340	                    "long_breakout_distance_pct",
   341	                    0.0,
   342	                )
   343	            )
   344	
   345	            # Normalized signal strength:
   346	            #
   347	            # 1.0 = strict signal
   348	            # 0.8 = full directional setup, waiting for breakout
   349	            # lower = incomplete setup
   350	            if strict_signal:
   351	                signal_strength = 1.0
   352	            elif setup_state == "SETUP_READY":
   353	                signal_strength = 0.8
   354	            elif setup_state == "WATCHING":
   355	                signal_strength = 0.5
   356	            else:
   357	                signal_strength = 0.0
   358	
   359	            risk_pct = self.stop_pct * 100.0
   360	
   361	            risk_score = max(
   362	                0.0,
   363	                min(
   364	                    20.0,
   365	                    20.0
   366	                    * (
   367	                        0.02
   368	                        / max(
   369	                            self.stop_pct,
   370	                            0.0001,
   371	                        )
   372	                    ),
   373	                ),
   374	            )
   375	
   376	            rr_score = min(
   377	                20.0,
   378	                20.0 * (rr / 3.0),
   379	            )
   380	
   381	            htf_score = 15.0 if htf else 0.0
   382	
   383	            atr_score = max(
   384	                0.0,
   385	                10.0
   386	                * (
   387	                    1.0
   388	                    - atr_pct
   389	                    / max(
   390	                        self.max_atr_pct,
   391	                        1e-9,
   392	                    )
   393	                ),
   394	            )
   395	
   396	            spread_score = max(
   397	                0.0,
   398	                5.0
   399	                * (
   400	                    1.0
   401	                    - spread_pct
   402	                    / max(
   403	                        self.max_spread_pct,
   404	                        1e-9,
   405	                    )
   406	                ),
   407	            )
   408	
   409	            # Strategy quality contributes the largest ranking
   410	            # component. This differentiates SOL/BNB/AVAX style
   411	            # setups instead of assigning every signal the same 30.
   412	            strategy_score = (
   413	                30.0
   414	                * (setup_score / 100.0)
   415	            )
   416	
   417	            # Strict breakout receives the final confirmation bonus.
   418	            breakout_bonus = (
   419	                15.0
   420	                if strict_signal
   421	                else 0.0
   422	            )
   423	
   424	            score = min(
   425	                100.0,
   426	                strategy_score
   427	                + risk_score
   428	                + rr_score
   429	                + htf_score
   430	                + atr_score
   431	                + spread_score
   432	                + breakout_bonus,
   433	            )
   434	
   435	            if strict_signal:
   436	                reason = (
   437	                    "strict long signal passed "
   438	                    "strategy and scanner filters"
   439	                )
   440	            elif setup_state == "SETUP_READY":
   441	                reason = (
   442	                    "bullish setup ready; "
   443	                    "waiting for strict fractal breakout"
   444	                )
   445	            else:
   446	                reason = (
   447	                    "bullish setup being monitored"
   448	                )
   449	
   450	            return Candidate(
   451	                symbol=symbol,
   452	                score=round(score, 2),
   453	                signal=strict_signal,
   454	                setup_score=round(
   455	                    setup_score,
   456	                    2,
   457	                ),
   458	                signal_strength=round(
   459	                    signal_strength,
   460	                    3,
   461	                ),
   462	                breakout_distance_pct=round(
   463	                    breakout_distance_pct,
   464	                    4,
   465	                ),
   466	                risk_pct=round(
   467	                    risk_pct,
   468	                    4,
   469	                ),
   470	                risk_reward=round(
   471	                    rr,
   472	                    3,
   473	                ),
   474	                atr_pct=round(
   475	                    atr_pct,
   476	                    6,
   477	                ),
   478	                spread_pct=round(
   479	                    spread_pct,
   480	                    6,
   481	                ),
   482	                htf_confirmed=htf,
   483	                setup_state=setup_state,
   484	                reason=reason,
   485	            )
   486	
   487	        except Exception as exc:
   488	            log.warning(
   489	                "Scanner skipped %s: %s",
   490	                symbol,
   491	                exc,
   492	            )
   493	            return None
   494	
   495	    @staticmethod
   496	    def _ranking_key(candidate):
   497	        """
   498	        Strict signals always outrank merely watched setups.
   499	        Within the same state, quality/risk determine the winner.
   500	        """
   501	
   502	        return (
   503	            1 if candidate.signal else 0,
   504	            candidate.score,
   505	            candidate.setup_score,
   506	            candidate.risk_reward,
   507	            -candidate.risk_pct,
   508	            -candidate.spread_pct,
   509	        )
   510	
   511	    def scan(self) -> List[Candidate]:
   512	        candidates = []
   513	
   514	        log.info(
   515	            "AUTO-SCAN START: symbols=%s interval=%s",
   516	            ",".join(self.symbols),
   517	            self.interval,
   518	        )
   519	
   520	        for symbol in self.symbols:
   521	            candidate = self.analyse(symbol)
   522	
   523	            if candidate is not None:
   524	                candidates.append(candidate)
   525	                log.info(
   526	                    "AUTO-SCAN CANDIDATE: %s state=%s strict=%s score=%.2f",
   527	                    candidate.symbol,
   528	                    candidate.setup_state,
   529	                    candidate.signal,
   530	                    candidate.score,
   531	                )
   532	            else:
   533	                log.info(
   534	                    "AUTO-SCAN SKIP: %s",
   535	                    symbol,
   536	                )
   537	
   538	        candidates.sort(
   539	            key=self._ranking_key,
   540	            reverse=True,
   541	        )
   542	
   543	        log.info(
   544	            "AUTO-SCAN END: candidates=%d selected=%s",
   545	            len(candidates),
   546	            candidates[0].symbol if candidates else "NONE",
   547	        )
   548	
   549	        return candidates
   550	
   551	    def best(self) -> Optional[Candidate]:
   552	        candidates = self.scan()
   553	
   554	        if not candidates:
   555	            return None
   556	
   557	        return max(
   558	            candidates,
   559	            key=self._ranking_key,
   560	        )

     1	import os, threading, time, asyncio, json
     2	from datetime import datetime, timezone

     4	from dotenv import load_dotenv
     5	from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
     6	from pydantic import BaseModel
     7	from db import Database
     8	from trader import Trader
     9	from data import fetch_klines

    15	load_dotenv(); API_TOKEN=os.getenv('MOBILE_API_TOKEN','').strip(); VERSION='4.11.0'
    16	app=FastAPI(title='Williams Binance Bot API',version=VERSION); hub=WebSocketHub()
    17	class CredentialPayload(BaseModel): api_key:str; api_secret:str; testnet:bool=True
    18	class ControlState:
    19	    def __init__(self):
    20	        self.lock=threading.RLock(); self.trader=None; self.thread=None; self.running=False; self.paused=False; self.last_error=None; self.api_key=''; self.api_secret=''; self.testnet=True
    21	        self.credentials=CredentialStore()
    22	        stored=self.credentials.load()
    23	        if stored:
    24	            self.api_key=stored['api_key']; self.api_secret=stored['api_secret']; self.testnet=stored['testnet']
    25	    def configure(self,key,secret,testnet=True):
    26	        with self.lock:
    27	            key=key.strip(); secret=secret.strip(); testnet=bool(testnet)
    28	            if self.running and (key!=self.api_key or secret!=self.api_secret or testnet!=self.testnet):
    29	                raise RuntimeError('Stop the bot before changing Binance credentials.')
    30	            if self.running and key==self.api_key and secret==self.api_secret and testnet==self.testnet:
    31	                return
    32	            self.api_key=key; self.api_secret=secret; self.testnet=testnet; self.trader=None
    33	            self.credentials.save(self.api_key,self.api_secret,self.testnet)
    34	        hub.configure_credentials(self.api_key,self.api_secret,self.testnet)
    35	    def ensure_trader(self):
    36	        with self.lock:
    37	            if self.trader is None:self.trader=Trader(api_key=self.api_key or None,api_secret=self.api_secret or None,testnet=self.testnet)
    38	            return self.trader
    39	    def loop(self):
    40	        t=self.ensure_trader()
    41	        try:
    42	            t.setup();
    43	            with self.lock:self.running=True; self.last_error=None
    44	            while self.running:
    45	                if not self.paused:
    46	                    try:t.process()
    47	                    except Exception as e:self.last_error=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'; t.db.log_event('ERROR','api_loop_error',self.last_error)
    48	                time.sleep(t.poll_seconds)
    49	        except Exception as e:self.last_error=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
    50	        finally:
    51	            with self.lock:self.running=False
    52	    def start(self):
    53	        with self.lock:
    54	            if self.thread is not None and self.thread.is_alive(): return False
    55	            self.paused=False; self.thread=threading.Thread(target=self.loop,daemon=True,name='williams-trader'); self.thread.start(); return True
    56	    def stop(self):
    57	        with self.lock:self.running=False; self.paused=False; thread=self.thread
    58	        if thread is not None and thread.is_alive() and thread is not threading.current_thread():thread.join(timeout=5)
    59	        return True
    60	    def pause(self):
    61	        with self.lock:self.paused=True
    62	        return True
    63	    def resume(self):
    64	        with self.lock:self.paused=False
    65	        return True
    66	state=ControlState()
    67	
    68	scanner_lock = threading.Lock()
    69	scanner_cache = {
    70	    "time": 0.0,
    71	    "data": [],
    72	    "scanning": False,
    73	    "last_error": None,
    74	}
    75	SCANNER_CACHE_SECONDS = max(
    76	    10,
    77	    int(os.getenv("SCANNER_CACHE_SECONDS", "30"))
    78	)
    79	
    80	def _scanner_worker():
    81	    global scanner_cache
    82	
    83	    if not scanner_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
    84	        return
    85	
    86	    try:
    87	        scanner_cache["scanning"] = True
    88	        scanner_cache["last_error"] = None
    89	
    90	        t = state.ensure_trader()
    91	        results = MarketScanner(t.client).scan()
    92	        data = [candidate.to_dict() for candidate in results]
    93	
    94	        scanner_cache["time"] = time.time()
    95	        scanner_cache["data"] = data
    96	    except Exception as exc:
    97	        scanner_cache["last_error"] = (
    98	            f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
    99	        )
   100	    finally:
   101	        scanner_cache["scanning"] = False
   102	        scanner_lock.release()
   103	
   104	def _start_scanner_background():
   105	    if scanner_cache["scanning"]:
   106	        return False
   107	
   108	    thread = threading.Thread(
   109	        target=_scanner_worker,
   110	        daemon=True,
   111	        name="williams-scanner",
   112	    )
   113	    thread.start()
   114	    return True
   115	@app.on_event('startup')

   120	        state.start()
   121	@app.on_event('shutdown')

   123	def auth(authorization:Optional[str]=Header(None)):
   124	    if len(API_TOKEN)<32:raise HTTPException(503,'MOBILE_API_TOKEN is not configured or is too short (minimum 32 characters).')
   125	    if authorization!=f'Bearer {API_TOKEN}':raise HTTPException(401,'Unauthorized')
   126	def db():return state.ensure_trader().db

   129	@app.post('/api/v1/config/binance',dependencies=[Depends(auth)])
   130	def configure(payload:CredentialPayload):
   131	    if not payload.api_key or not payload.api_secret:raise HTTPException(400,'API key and secret are required')
   132	    try:state.configure(payload.api_key,payload.api_secret,payload.testnet)
   133	    except RuntimeError as e:raise HTTPException(409,str(e))
   134	    return {'configured':True,'testnet':payload.testnet}
   135	@app.delete('/api/v1/config/binance',dependencies=[Depends(auth)])
   136	def clear_binance_config():
   137	    state.stop()
   138	    with state.lock:state.api_key='';state.api_secret='';state.trader=None;state.last_error=None;state.paused=False
   139	    state.credentials.clear(); hub.configure_credentials('','',True);return {'configured':False,'cleared':True}
   140	@app.websocket('/api/v1/ws')
   141	async def realtime_ws(websocket:WebSocket):
   142	    if len(API_TOKEN)<32 or websocket.headers.get('authorization')!=f'Bearer {API_TOKEN}':await websocket.close(1008);return
   143	    await websocket.accept(); client=type('RealtimeClient',(),{})();client.websocket=websocket;client.loop=asyncio.get_running_loop();client.queue=asyncio.Queue();hub.add_client(client);sender=asyncio.create_task(_ws_sender(client))
   144	    try:
   145	        while True:
   146	            msg=await websocket.receive_text()
   147	            if msg.lower()=='ping':await websocket.send_text('{"type":"pong"}')
   148	            elif msg.lower()=='snapshot':await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({'type':'snapshot','data':hub.snapshot()},separators=(',',':')))
   149	    except WebSocketDisconnect:pass
   150	    finally:sender.cancel();hub.remove_client(client)
   151	async def _ws_sender(client):
   152	    while True:await client.websocket.send_text(json.dumps(await client.queue.get(),separators=(',',':')))
   153	@app.get('/api/v1/status',dependencies=[Depends(auth)])
   154	def status():
   155	    t=state.ensure_trader();ticker=balance=position=None
   156	    if t.client.api_key and t.client.api_secret:
   157	        try:
   158	            ticker=float(t.client.ticker_price(t.symbol)['price']);balance=t.available_quote();trade=t.db.open_trade()
   159	            if trade:
   160	                qty=t._trade_remaining_qty(trade,t.client.all_orders(t.symbol,limit=1000))
   161	                if qty>=t._min_qty():position={'side':'LONG','quantity':qty,'entry_price':float(trade['entry_price'])}
   162	        except Exception as e:state.last_error=f'status: {e}'
   163	    pnl=pnl_pct=None;tp=sl=None
   164	    if position and ticker is not None:
   165	        entry=position['entry_price'];qty=position['quantity'];pnl=(ticker-entry)*qty;pnl_pct=ticker/entry-1 if entry else None
   166	        for o in db().recent_orders(t.symbol,100):
   167	            if str(o.get('status','')).upper() not in {'NEW','PENDING_NEW','PARTIALLY_FILLED'}:continue
   168	            typ=str(o.get('type','')).upper()
   169	            if 'TAKE_PROFIT' in typ and o.get('price'):tp=float(o['price'])
   170	            elif 'STOP_LOSS' in typ:sl=float(o.get('stop_price') or o.get('price') or 0) or None
   171	    return {'version':VERSION,'symbol':t.symbol,'interval':t.interval,'testnet':t.client.testnet,'running':state.running,'paused':state.paused,'state':t.state(),'recovered':t.recovered,'last_error':state.last_error,'binance_configured':bool(t.client.api_key and t.client.api_secret),'price':ticker,'quote_balance':balance,'position':position,'pnl':pnl,'pnl_pct':pnl_pct,'take_profit_price':tp,'stop_loss_price':sl,'stop_loss_pct':t.stop_pct,'take_profit_pct':t.target_pct,'risk_per_trade_pct':t.risk_per_trade_pct,'max_daily_loss_pct':t.max_daily_loss_pct,'max_trades_per_day':t.max_trades_day,'consecutive_losses':t.db.consecutive_losses(t.symbol),'trades_today':t.db.trades_today(t.symbol),'server_time':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
   172	@app.get('/api/v1/scanner',dependencies=[Depends(auth)])
   173	def scanner(refresh:bool=False):
   174	    now = time.time()
   175	    age = (
   176	        round(now - scanner_cache["time"], 2)
   177	        if scanner_cache["time"]
   178	        else None
   179	    )
   180	
   181	    # Normal requests use the latest cached result.
   182	    if not refresh:
   183	        return {
   184	            "version": VERSION,
   185	            "cached": bool(scanner_cache["data"]),
   186	            "scanning": scanner_cache["scanning"],
   187	            "age_seconds": age,
   188	            "last_error": scanner_cache["last_error"],
   189	            "candidates": scanner_cache["data"],
   190	        }
   191	
   192	    # Manual refresh starts a background scan and returns immediately.
   193	    started = _start_scanner_background()
   194	
   195	    return {
   196	        "version": VERSION,
   197	        "cached": bool(scanner_cache["data"]),
   198	        "scanning": scanner_cache["scanning"],
   199	        "started": started,
   200	        "age_seconds": age,
   201	        "last_error": scanner_cache["last_error"],
   202	        "candidates": scanner_cache["data"],
   203	    }
   204	
   205	@app.get('/api/v1/market/klines',dependencies=[Depends(auth)])
   206	def market_klines(limit:int=120):
   207	    t=state.ensure_trader();df=fetch_klines(t.client,t.symbol,t.interval,limit=max(30,min(limit,250)));ind=calculate_indicators(df.iloc[:-1].copy(),config_from_env());rows=[]
   208	    for idx,row in ind.iterrows():rows.append({'time':idx.isoformat(),'open':float(row.open),'high':float(row.high),'low':float(row.low),'close':float(row.close),'jaw':None if row.jaw_shifted!=row.jaw_shifted else float(row.jaw_shifted),'teeth':None if row.teeth_shifted!=row.teeth_shifted else float(row.teeth_shifted),'lips':None if row.lips_shifted!=row.lips_shifted else float(row.lips_shifted),'ao':None if row.ao!=row.ao else float(row.ao),'long_signal':bool(row.long_signal),'fractal_up':bool(row.fractal_up),'fractal_down':bool(row.fractal_down)})
   209	    return {'symbol':t.symbol,'interval':t.interval,'candles':rows}

    22	        self.position_fraction=float(os.getenv('POSITION_FRACTION','0.25')); self.stop_pct=float(os.getenv('STOP_LOSS_PCT','0.02')); self.target_pct=float(os.getenv('TAKE_PROFIT_PCT','0.04'))
    23	        self.poll_seconds=int(os.getenv('POLL_SECONDS','20'))
    24	        self.risk_per_trade_pct=float(os.getenv('RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT','0.01')); self.max_daily_loss_pct=float(os.getenv('MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT','0.03'))
    25	        self.max_trades_day=int(os.getenv('MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY','5')); self.max_consecutive_losses=int(os.getenv('MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES','3')); self.cooldown_minutes=int(os.getenv('COOLDOWN_MINUTES','30'))
    26	        self.min_risk_reward=float(os.getenv('MIN_RISK_REWARD','1.5')); self.atr_period=int(os.getenv('ATR_PERIOD','14')); self.max_atr_pct=float(os.getenv('MAX_ATR_PCT','0.08'))
    27	        self.max_spread_pct=float(os.getenv('MAX_SPREAD_PCT','0.0015')); self.require_htf_confirmation=os.getenv('REQUIRE_HTF_CONFIRMATION','true').lower()=='true'; self.htf_interval=os.getenv('HTF_INTERVAL','4h')

    31	            or 'data/trader.sqlite3'
    32	        ); self.tg=Telegram(os.getenv('TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN',''),os.getenv('TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID',''))
    33	        self.client=BinanceSpotClient(api_key if api_key is not None else os.getenv('BINANCE_API_KEY',''), api_secret if api_secret is not None else os.getenv('BINANCE_API_SECRET',''), testnet=(os.getenv('TESTNET','true').lower()=='true') if testnet is None else testnet)
    34	        self.filters={}; self.base_asset=self.quote_asset=None; self.recovered=False
