    69	    def setup(self):
    70	        if not self.client.testnet and os.getenv('ALLOW_LIVE','false').lower()!='true': raise RuntimeError('LIVE trading is disabled. Set TESTNET=true or explicitly ALLOW_LIVE=true.')
    71	        if not self.client.api_key or not self.client.api_secret: raise RuntimeError('BINANCE_API_KEY and BINANCE_API_SECRET are required.')
    72	        self.client.sync_time(); info=self.client.exchange_info(self.symbol); s=info['symbols'][0]; self.filters={f['filterType']:f for f in s['filters']}; self.base_asset=s['baseAsset']; self.quote_asset=s['quoteAsset']
    73	        self.db.log_event('INFO','startup','Trader initialized',{'symbol':self.symbol,'interval':self.interval,'testnet':self.client.testnet}); self.ensure_foreign_base_balance_baseline(); self.recover_state()
    74	        self.notify(f'Williams STARTED\n{self.symbol} {self.interval}\nTESTNET={self.client.testnet}\nSTATE={self.state()}')
    75	
    76	    def switch_symbol(self, symbol):
    77	        """
    78	        Безопасно переключает рабочую торговую пару.
    79	
    80	        ВАЖНО:
    81	        - не создаёт ордеров;
    82	        - не отменяет ордера;
    83	        - не меняет позицию;
    84	        - разрешено только из FLAT;
    85	        - заново загружает Binance filters/assets;
    86	        - проверяет отсутствие незавершённой позиции новой пары.
    87	        """
    88	        new_symbol = str(symbol).upper().strip()
    89	
    90	        if not new_symbol:
    91	            raise ValueError('Symbol cannot be empty')
    92	
    93	        if self.state() != 'FLAT':
    94	            raise RuntimeError(
    95	                f'Cannot switch symbol while state={self.state()}'
    96	            )
    97	
    98	        # Проверяем символ непосредственно через Binance.
    99	        info = self.client.exchange_info(new_symbol)
   100	        symbols = info.get('symbols', [])
   101	
   102	        if not symbols:
   103	            raise RuntimeError(
   104	                f'Symbol {new_symbol} not found in Binance exchange info'
   105	            )
   106	
   107	        s = symbols[0]
   108	
   109	        if s.get('status') != 'TRADING':
   110	            raise RuntimeError(
   111	                f'Symbol {new_symbol} is not TRADING: {s.get("status")}'
   112	            )
   113	
   114	        base_asset = s.get('baseAsset')
   115	        quote_asset = s.get('quoteAsset')
   116	
   117	        if not base_asset or not quote_asset:
   118	            raise RuntimeError(
   119	                f'Invalid assets for {new_symbol}'
   120	            )
   121	
   122	        # HARD SINGLE-POSITION GUARD:
   123	        # Automatic scanner supports exactly one managed position.
   124	        # Therefore symbol switching is forbidden while ANY open trade
   125	        # exists, even if that trade belongs to another symbol.
   126	        any_open_trade = self.db.open_trade()
   127	
   128	        if any_open_trade is not None:
   129	            raise RuntimeError(
   130	                'Cannot switch symbol while a managed position exists: '
   131	                f"{any_open_trade.get('symbol')}"
   132	            )
   133	
   134	        # An unresolved entry intent is also a hard cross-symbol block.
   135	        # The exchange may have accepted the BUY even if its response
   136	        # was lost or delayed.
   137	        entry_intent = self.db.state_get('entry_client_order_id')
   138	
   139	        if entry_intent:
   140	            raise RuntimeError(
   141	                'Cannot switch symbol while an entry intent is unresolved: '
   142	                f'{entry_intent}'
   143	            )
   144	
   145	        # No unfinished position for the target symbol.
   146	        # This remains as an explicit per-symbol guard even though the
   147	        # global single-position guard above already covers it.
   148	        if self.db.open_trade(new_symbol) is not None:
   149	            raise RuntimeError(
   150	                f'Open trade already exists for {new_symbol}'
   151	            )
   152	
   153	        # Загружаем filters только после успешной проверки.
   154	        filters = {
   155	            f['filterType']: f
   156	            for f in s.get('filters', [])
   157	        }
   158	
   159	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   160	        # ATOMIC RUNTIME SWITCH
   161	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   162	        # Everything above this point is preparation only.
   163	        # No Trader runtime state has been changed yet.
   164	        #
   165	        # The commit below is protected by a rollback so a failure in
   166	        # DB state/event persistence cannot leave Trader half-switched.
   167	
   168	        old_symbol = self.symbol
   169	        old_active_symbol = self.active_symbol
   170	        old_filters = self.filters
   171	        old_base_asset = self.base_asset
   172	        old_quote_asset = self.quote_asset
   173	        old_recovered = self.recovered
   174	
   175	        try:
   176	            # TRUE DB TRANSACTION:
   177	            # active_symbol + symbol_switched event are committed together.
   178	            # If anything fails, SQLite rolls both changes back.
   179	            with self.db.transaction():
   180	                self.symbol = new_symbol
   181	                self.active_symbol = new_symbol
   182	                self.filters = filters
   183	                self.base_asset = base_asset
   184	                self.quote_asset = quote_asset
   185	                self.recovered = False
   186	
   187	                self.db.state_set('active_symbol', new_symbol)
   188	
   189	                self.db.log_event(
   190	                    'INFO',
   191	                    'symbol_switched',
   192	                    'Trader working symbol switched',
   193	                    {
   194	                        'from': old_symbol,
   195	                        'to': new_symbol,
   196	                        'base_asset': base_asset,
   197	                        'quote_asset': quote_asset,
   198	                    }
   199	                )
   200	
   201	        except Exception:
   202	            # Runtime rollback.
   203	            # DB rollback is already handled by Database.transaction().
   204	            self.symbol = old_symbol
   205	            self.active_symbol = old_active_symbol
   206	            self.filters = old_filters
   207	            self.base_asset = old_base_asset
   208	            self.quote_asset = old_quote_asset
   209	            self.recovered = old_recovered
   210	            raise
   211	
   212	        return {
   213	            'symbol': self.symbol,
   214	            'base_asset': self.base_asset,
   215	            'quote_asset': self.quote_asset,
   216	            'status': s.get('status'),
   217	            'filters': self.filters,
   218	        }
   219	
   220	    def state(self): return self.db.state_get('position_state','FLAT')
   221	    def _set_state(self,state):
   222	        if state not in STATES: raise ValueError(f'Unknown state {state}')
   223	        self.db.state_set('position_state',state)
   224	
   225	    def normalize_qty(self,qty):
   226	        f=self.filters.get('LOT_SIZE') or self.filters.get('MARKET_LOT_SIZE'); step=f['stepSize'] if f else '0.000001'; min_qty=float(f['minQty']) if f else 0; q=self.client.decimal_floor(qty,step); return float(q) if float(q)>=min_qty else 0.0
   227	    def normalize_price(self,price):
   228	        f=self.filters.get('PRICE_FILTER'); tick=f['tickSize'] if f else '0.01'; return float(self.client.decimal_floor(price,tick))
   229	    def _ensure_symbol_assets(self):
   230	        if self.base_asset and self.quote_asset:
   231	            return
   232	        info=self.client.exchange_info(self.symbol)
   233	        symbols=info.get('symbols',[])
   234	        if not symbols:
   235	            raise RuntimeError(f'Symbol {self.symbol} not found in Binance exchange info')
   236	        s=symbols[0]
   237	        self.base_asset=s.get('baseAsset')
   238	        self.quote_asset=s.get('quoteAsset')
   239	        if not self.quote_asset:
   240	            raise RuntimeError(f'Quote asset not found for {self.symbol}')
   241	
   242	    def available_quote(self):
   243	        self._ensure_symbol_assets()
   244	        a=self.client.account()
   245	        return next((float(b['free']) for b in a.get('balances',[]) if b.get('asset')==self.quote_asset),0.0)
   246	    def base_balance_total(self):
   247	        self._ensure_symbol_assets()
   248	        a=self.client.account()
   249	        return next((float(b['free'])+float(b['locked']) for b in a.get('balances',[]) if b.get('asset')==self.base_asset),0.0)
   250	
   251	    def bot_base_balance(self, total_balance=None):
   252	        if total_balance is None:
   253	            total_balance=self.base_balance_total()
   254	
   255	        key = f'foreign_base_balance:{self.symbol}'
   256	        baseline = self.db.state_get(key)
   257	
   258	        # Backward compatibility:
   259	        # older databases/tests used the global foreign_base_balance key.
   260	        #
   261	        # IMPORTANT:
   262	        # A global legacy baseline has no symbol information. It must
   263	        # never be copied blindly to a newly selected symbol.
   264	        #
   265	        # It is migrated only during the original active-symbol context.
   266	        # After migration the legacy key is removed, so it cannot leak
   267	        # into another symbol later.
   268	        if baseline is None:
   269	            legacy = self.db.state_get('foreign_base_balance')
   270	            active_symbol = self.db.state_get('active_symbol')
   271	
   272	            if (
   273	                legacy is not None
   274	                and active_symbol
   275	                and str(active_symbol).upper() == self.symbol
   276	            ):
   277	                baseline = float(legacy)
   278	                self.db.state_set(key, baseline)
   279	                self.db.state_delete('foreign_base_balance')
   280	
   281	        if baseline is None:
   282	            raise RuntimeError(
   283	                f'foreign_base_balance baseline is not initialized for {self.symbol}'
   284	            )
   285	
   286	        return max(0.0, float(total_balance)-float(baseline))
   287	
   288	    def ensure_foreign_base_balance_baseline(self, total_balance=None):
   289	        key = f'foreign_base_balance:{self.symbol}'
   290	        baseline = self.db.state_get(key)
   291	        if baseline is not None:
   292	            return float(baseline)
   293	
   294	        # Migrate the legacy global baseline only when it clearly
   295	        # belongs to the currently active symbol.
   296	        #
   297	        # A global baseline has no symbol information, therefore it must
   298	        # never be copied to a newly selected pair.
   299	        legacy = self.db.state_get('foreign_base_balance')
   300	        active_symbol = self.db.state_get('active_symbol')
   301	
   302	        if (
   303	            legacy is not None
   304	            and active_symbol
   305	            and str(active_symbol).upper() == self.symbol
   306	        ):
   307	            baseline = float(legacy)
   308	            self.db.state_set(key, baseline)
   309	            self.db.state_delete('foreign_base_balance')
   310	            return baseline
   311	
   312	        if total_balance is None:
   313	            total_balance=self.base_balance_total()
   314	
   315	        open_trade=self.db.open_trade(self.symbol)
   316	        entry_intent=self.db.state_get('entry_client_order_id')
   317	
   318	        if open_trade is None and not entry_intent and self.state() in {'FLAT','RECONCILE_REQUIRED'}:
   319	            self.db.state_set(f'foreign_base_balance:{self.symbol}', float(total_balance))
   320	            self.db.log_event(
   321	                'INFO',
   322	                'foreign_balance_baselined',
   323	                'Captured pre-existing base-asset balance',
   324	                {'asset':self.base_asset,'balance':float(total_balance)}
   325	            )
   326	            return float(total_balance)
   327	
   328	        raise RuntimeError(
   329	            'Cannot initialize foreign_base_balance while a managed position or unresolved entry exists'
   330	        )
   331	    def _min_qty(self):
   332	        f=self.filters.get('LOT_SIZE') or self.filters.get('MARKET_LOT_SIZE'); return float(f['minQty']) if f else 0.0
   333	    def _min_notional(self):
   334	        f=self.filters.get('NOTIONAL') or self.filters.get('MIN_NOTIONAL') or {}; return float(f.get('minNotional',0) or 0)
   335	    def _is_bot_order(self,o): return str(o.get('clientOrderId','')).startswith(('WILLV4_ENTRY_','WILLV4_OCO_'))
   336	    def _is_bot_oco_list(self,lst):
   337	        lid=str(lst.get('listClientOrderId',''))
   338	        return lid.startswith('WILLV4_OCO_') or any(str(o.get('clientOrderId','')).startswith(('WILLV4_OCO_','tp-','sl-')) for o in lst.get('orders',[]))
   339	    def _sell_belongs_to_bot(self,o,all_orders):
   340	        cid=str(o.get('clientOrderId','')); lid=str(o.get('orderListId',''))
   341	        if cid.startswith(('WILLV4_OCO_','tp-','sl-')): return True
   342	        if not lid:return False
   343	        if any(str(x.get('orderListId',''))==lid and str(x.get('clientOrderId','')).startswith(('WILLV4_OCO_','tp-','sl-')) for x in all_orders):return True
   344	        return self.db.conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM orders WHERE order_list_id=? AND client_order_id LIKE 'WILLV4_OCO_%' LIMIT 1",(lid,)).fetchone() is not None
   345	
   346	    def _filled_sell_qty_after(self,buy,all_orders):
   347	        bt=int(buy.get('time',buy.get('transactTime',0)) or 0)
   348	        return sum(float(o.get('executedQty',0) or 0) for o in all_orders if o.get('side')=='SELL' and o.get('status')=='FILLED' and int(o.get('time',0) or 0)>=bt and self._sell_belongs_to_bot(o,all_orders))
   349	
   350	    def _trade_remaining_qty(self,trade,all_orders):
   351	        entry_id=str(trade.get('entry_order_id') or ''); buy=next((o for o in all_orders if str(o.get('orderId'))==entry_id and o.get('side')=='BUY'),None)
   352	        if not buy:return float(trade.get('quantity') or 0)
   353	        bought=float(buy.get('executedQty',0) or 0); sold=self._filled_sell_qty_after(buy,all_orders)
   354	        return max(0.0,min(float(trade.get('quantity') or bought),bought)-sold)
   355	
   356	    def _recover_entry_intent(self,all_orders):
   357	        cid=self.db.state_get('entry_client_order_id')
   358	        if not cid:return
   359	        matches=[o for o in all_orders if str(o.get('clientOrderId'))==cid]
   360	        if not matches:return
   361	        order=matches[-1]; self.db.save_order(order)
   362	        status=str(order.get('status','')).upper()
   363	        if status=='FILLED':
   364	            qty=float(order.get('executedQty',0) or 0)
   365	            spent=float(order.get('cummulativeQuoteQty',0) or 0)
   366	            entry=spent/qty if spent and qty else float(order.get('price',0) or 0)
   367	
   368	            # HARD RECOVERY GUARD:
   369	            # A confirmed BUY that is too small to represent a valid
   370	            # Williams position must never silently become FLAT.
   371	            if qty < self._min_qty():
   372	                self.db.state_delete('entry_client_order_id')
   373	                self._set_state('RECONCILE_REQUIRED')
   374	                self.db.log_event(
   375	                    'ERROR',
   376	                    'invalid_recovered_entry',
   377	                    f'Exchange BUY is FILLED but quantity {qty} is below minimum {self._min_qty()}; manual reconciliation required',
   378	                    order
   379	                )
   380	                print(
   381	                    f'[RECOVERY] FILLED BUY below minQty -> RECONCILE_REQUIRED: '
   382	                    f'{qty} < {self._min_qty()}'
   383	                )
   384	                return
   385	
   386	            if self.db.open_trade(self.symbol) is None:
   387	                self.db.save_trade(
   388	                    entry_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(
   389	                        int(order.get('transactTime',order.get('time',0)))/1000,
   390	                        tz=timezone.utc
   391	                    ).isoformat(),
   392	                    symbol=self.symbol,
   393	                    side='LONG',
   394	                    entry_price=entry,
   395	                    quantity=qty,
   396	                    entry_order_id=str(order.get('orderId')),
   397	                    fees=0
   398	                )
   399	
   400	            self.db.state_delete('entry_client_order_id')
   401	            self._set_state('EXIT_PENDING')
   402	            self.db.log_event(
   403	                'WARNING',
   404	                'entry_recovered',
   405	                'Recovered an entry that may have filled before the client received the response; OCO recovery required',
   406	                order
   407	            )
   408	        elif status in {'CANCELED','REJECTED','EXPIRED'}:
   409	            self.db.state_delete('entry_client_order_id'); self._set_state('FLAT')
   410	
   411	    def recover_state(self):
   412	        self.db.log_event('INFO','recovery_start','Starting exchange/SQLite reconciliation')
   413	        open_trade=self.db.open_trade(self.symbol); all_orders=self.client.all_orders(self.symbol,limit=1000)
   414	        for o in all_orders:self.db.save_order(o)
   415	        self._recover_entry_intent(all_orders); open_trade=self.db.open_trade(self.symbol)
   416	        open_lists=self.client.open_order_lists(self.symbol)
   417	        for lst in open_lists:
   418	            for o in lst.get('orders',[]):self.db.save_order(o)
   419	        bot_open_lists=[x for x in open_lists if self._is_bot_oco_list(x)]
   420	        open_oco_ids={str(x.get('orderListId')) for x in bot_open_lists if x.get('orderListId') is not None}
   421	        position_qty=self.base_balance_total()
   422	
   423	        # A fresh database has no foreign-balance baseline yet.
   424	        # Never assume zero: that could misclassify an existing user-owned
   425	        # base-asset balance as a Williams position.
   426	        #
   427	        # We can safely initialize the baseline only when there is no
   428	        # managed position, no unresolved entry intent, and no unresolved
   429	        # Williams BUY remaining on the exchange.
   430	        baseline_key=f'foreign_base_balance:{self.symbol}'
   431	        baseline=self.db.state_get(baseline_key)
   432	
   433	        if baseline is None:
   434	            entry_intent=self.db.state_get('entry_client_order_id')
   435	
   436	            unresolved_bot_buy=False
   437	            for buy in all_orders:
   438	                if (
   439	                    buy.get('side') == 'BUY'
   440	                    and buy.get('status') == 'FILLED'
   441	                    and self._is_bot_order(buy)
   442	                ):
   443	                    bought=float(buy.get('executedQty',0) or 0)
   444	                    sold=self._filled_sell_qty_after(buy,all_orders)
   445	                    remaining=max(0.0,bought-sold)
   446	
   447	                    if remaining >= self._min_qty():
   448	                        unresolved_bot_buy=True
   449	                        break
   450	
   451	            if open_trade is None and not entry_intent and not unresolved_bot_buy:
   452	                self.ensure_foreign_base_balance_baseline(position_qty)
   453	                baseline=self.db.state_get(baseline_key)
   454	            else:
   455	                self._set_state('RECONCILE_REQUIRED')
   456	                self.recovered=True
   457	                self.db.log_event(
   458	                    'ERROR',
   459	                    'recovery_baseline_missing',
   460	                    'Recovery blocked because foreign base balance baseline is missing while managed exchange activity exists',
   461	                    {
   462	                        'symbol':self.symbol,
   463	                        'total_balance':float(position_qty),
   464	                        'open_trade':bool(open_trade),
   465	                        'entry_intent':bool(entry_intent),
   466	                        'unresolved_bot_buy':bool(unresolved_bot_buy)
   467	                    }
   468	                )
   469	                return
   470	
   471	        bot_position_qty=self.bot_base_balance(position_qty)
   472	        if open_trade is None:
   473	            candidates=[]
   474	            for buy in all_orders:
   475	                if buy.get('side')!='BUY' or buy.get('status')!='FILLED' or not self._is_bot_order(buy):continue
   476	                bought=float(buy.get('executedQty',0) or 0); sold=self._filled_sell_qty_after(buy,all_orders); remaining=max(0,bought-sold)
   477	                if remaining>=self._min_qty() and bot_position_qty>=self._min_qty():candidates.append((int(buy.get('time',buy.get('transactTime',0)) or 0),buy,remaining))
   478	            if candidates:
   479	                _,buy,remaining=max(candidates,key=lambda x:x[0]); quote=float(buy.get('cummulativeQuoteQty',0) or 0); bought=float(buy.get('executedQty',0) or 0); entry=quote/bought if quote and bought else float(buy.get('price',0) or 0); qty=min(remaining,bot_position_qty)
   480	                self.db.save_trade(entry_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(int(buy.get('transactTime',buy.get('time',0)))/1000,tz=timezone.utc).isoformat(),symbol=self.symbol,side='LONG',entry_price=entry,quantity=qty,entry_order_id=str(buy.get('orderId')),fees=0); open_trade=self.db.open_trade(self.symbol); self.db.log_event('WARNING','trade_reconstructed','Reconstructed unresolved bot-owned LONG',buy)
   481	        if open_trade:
   482	            expected_qty=self._trade_remaining_qty(open_trade,all_orders)
   483	            tolerance=max(self._min_qty(),expected_qty*float(os.getenv('BALANCE_TOLERANCE_PCT','0.002')))
   484	            if expected_qty < self._min_qty(): self._recover_closed_trade(open_trade,all_orders); self._set_state('FLAT')
   485	            elif abs(bot_position_qty-expected_qty)>tolerance:
   486	                self._set_state('RECONCILE_REQUIRED'); self.notify(f'RECOVERY BLOCKED\nExpected Williams {expected_qty:.8f} {self.base_asset}; actual Williams balance {bot_position_qty:.8f}. Total account balance={position_qty:.8f}; foreign baseline={position_qty-bot_position_qty:.8f}. Manual reconciliation required.'); self.recovered=True; return
   487	            else:
   488	                if not open_oco_ids:
   489	                    qty=min(expected_qty,bot_position_qty)
   490	                    self._set_state('EXIT_PENDING')
   491	                    try:
   492	                        self.place_oco(
   493	                            qty,
   494	                            float(open_trade['entry_price']),
   495	                            trade_id=open_trade['id']
   496	                        )
   497	                    except Exception as e:
   498	                        self._set_state('RECONCILE_REQUIRED')
   499	                        try:
   500	                            self.db.log_event(
   501	                                'ERROR',
   502	                                'oco_recovery_failed',
   503	                                'Failed to recreate OCO during recovery',
   504	                                {
   505	                                    'symbol': self.symbol,
   506	                                    'trade_id': open_trade['id'],
   507	                                    'error': str(e),
   508	                                }
   509	                            )
   510	                        except Exception:
   511	                            pass
   512	                        print('[RECOVERY] OCO recreation failed -> RECONCILE_REQUIRED:', e)
   513	                if self.state() != 'RECONCILE_REQUIRED':
   514	                    self._set_state('OPEN')
   515	        else:
   516	            # Never allow a foreign balance to create a bot position. An unresolved
   517	            # entry intent is also a hard block: the exchange may have accepted the
   518	            # BUY even if it is temporarily absent from the history response.
   519	            if self.state()=='ENTRY_PENDING':
   520	                self._set_state('RECONCILE_REQUIRED')
   521	                self.notify('RECOVERY BLOCKED\nAn entry request has no confirmed exchange result. Manual reconciliation is required before trading can resume.')
   522	            elif self.state()=='RECONCILE_REQUIRED':
   523	                pass
   524	            else:
   525	                self._set_state('FLAT')
   526	        self.recovered=True; self.db.log_event('INFO','recovery_complete','Exchange/SQLite reconciliation complete',{'position_qty':position_qty,'bot_trade':bool(open_trade),'open_oco_ids':sorted(open_oco_ids),'state':self.state()})
   527	
   528	    def _recover_closed_trade(self,trade,all_orders):
   529	        entry_id=str(trade.get('entry_order_id') or ''); entry_time=int(next((b.get('time',b.get('transactTime',0)) for b in all_orders if str(b.get('orderId'))==entry_id),0) or 0)
   530	        sells=[o for o in all_orders if o.get('side')=='SELL' and o.get('status')=='FILLED' and int(o.get('time',0) or 0)>=entry_time and self._sell_belongs_to_bot(o,all_orders)]
   531	        if not sells:return
   532	        # Prefer the trade's recorded OCO list; otherwise use the latest bot-owned exit execution.
   533	        target_lid=str(trade.get('exit_order_list_id') or '')
   534	        if target_lid:sells=[o for o in sells if str(o.get('orderListId'))==target_lid] or sells
   535	        sell=max(sells,key=lambda x:int(x.get('time',x.get('transactTime',0)) or 0)); qty=float(sell.get('executedQty',0) or 0); proceeds=float(sell.get('cummulativeQuoteQty',0) or 0); exit_price=proceeds/qty if qty else float(sell.get('price',0) or 0)
   536	        entry=float(trade['entry_price']); pnl=(exit_price-entry)*min(qty,float(trade['quantity'])); pct=(exit_price/entry-1) if entry else 0
   537	        self.db.close_trade(trade['id'],datetime.fromtimestamp(int(sell.get('transactTime',sell.get('time',0)))/1000,tz=timezone.utc).isoformat(),exit_price,pnl,pct,'TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS (recovered)',sell.get('orderListId')); self.notify(f'RECOVERY\nPosition closed offline\nexit≈{exit_price:.8f}\nPnL≈{pnl:.8f} ({pct:.2%})')
   538	
   539	    def _spread_pct(self):
   540	        b=self.client.book_ticker(self.symbol); bid=float(b['bidPrice']); ask=float(b['askPrice']); mid=(bid+ask)/2
   541	        return (ask-bid)/mid if mid else 1.0
   542	
   543	    @staticmethod
   544	    def _atr(df,period=14):
   545	        prev=df['close'].shift(1); tr=__import__('pandas').concat([df['high']-df['low'],(df['high']-prev).abs(),(df['low']-prev).abs()],axis=1).max(axis=1); return float(tr.rolling(period).mean().iloc[-1])
   546	
   547	    def _risk_gate(self, closed):
   548	        if self.state()=='RECONCILE_REQUIRED': return False,'RECONCILE_REQUIRED'
   549	        if self.state()!='FLAT': return False,f'state={self.state()}'
   550	        if self.db.trades_today(self.symbol)>=self.max_trades_day:return False,'max daily trades reached'
   551	        if self.db.consecutive_losses(self.symbol)>=self.max_consecutive_losses:return False,'max consecutive losses reached'
   552	        today_pnl=self.db.pnl_today(self.symbol); balance=max(self.available_quote(),0.0)
   553	        if balance>0 and today_pnl <= -balance*self.max_daily_loss_pct:return False,'daily loss limit reached'
   554	        last_exit=self.db.last_exit_time(self.symbol)
   555	        if last_exit:
   556	            try:
   557	                dt=datetime.fromisoformat(last_exit.replace('Z','+00:00'))
   558	                if datetime.now(timezone.utc)-dt < timedelta(minutes=self.cooldown_minutes):return False,'cooldown active'
   559	            except ValueError:pass
   560	        if self.target_pct/max(self.stop_pct,1e-9) < self.min_risk_reward:return False,'risk/reward below minimum'
   561	        spread=self._spread_pct()
   562	        if spread>self.max_spread_pct:return False,f'spread {spread:.4%} > {self.max_spread_pct:.4%}'
   563	        atr=self._atr(closed,self.atr_period); price=float(closed['close'].iloc[-1])
   564	        if not price or atr/price>self.max_atr_pct:return False,f'volatility too high: ATR {atr/price:.2%}'
   565	        if self.require_htf_confirmation:
   566	            htf=fetch_klines(self.client,self.symbol,self.htf_interval,limit=160)
   567	            if len(htf)<80:return False,'insufficient higher-timeframe data'
   568	            hi=calculate_indicators(htf.iloc[:-1].copy(),config_from_env()).iloc[-1]
   569	            if not bool(hi.get('bullish_alligator',False)) or float(hi.get('ao',0) or 0)<=0:return False,'higher-timeframe trend not confirmed'
   570	        return True,'ok'
   571	
   572	    def _position_quote(self,entry_price):
   573	        balance=self.available_quote(); cap=balance*self.position_fraction
   574	        risk_quote=balance*self.risk_per_trade_pct/max(self.stop_pct,1e-9)
   575	        return max(0.0,min(cap,risk_quote))
   576	
   577	    def market_buy(self,quote):
   578	        # HARD SAFETY:
   579	        # DRY_RUN must block BEFORE any DB mutation, entry intent,
   580	        # state transition, or exchange order request.
   581	        if self.dry_run:
   582	            raise RuntimeError(
   583	                'BUY blocked: DRY_RUN=true. '
   584	                'No live order execution is permitted.'
   585	            )
   586	
   587	        # Persist the currently selected symbol only after the immutable
   588	        # execution safety gate above has passed.
   589	        self.db.state_set('active_symbol', self.symbol)
   590	
   591	        # HARD ENTRY GUARD:
   592	        # A BUY may only start from a completely reconciled FLAT state.
   593	        current_state = self.state()
   594	        if current_state != 'FLAT':
   595	            raise RuntimeError(
   596	                f'BUY blocked: invalid state={current_state}. '
   597	                'Only FLAT may start a new entry.'
   598	            )
   599	
   600	        if quote<=0 or quote<self._min_notional():
   601	            raise RuntimeError(
   602	                f'Insufficient quote balance: quote={quote}, '
   603	                f'minNotional={self._min_notional()}'
   604	            )
   605	
   606	        cid=f'WILLV4_ENTRY_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:20]}'
   607	        self.db.state_set('entry_client_order_id',cid)
   608	        self._set_state('ENTRY_PENDING')
   609	
   610	        try:
   611	            order=self.client.order(
   612	                self.symbol,
   613	                'BUY',
   614	                'MARKET',
   615	                quote_order_qty=self.client.decimal_format(quote),
   616	                new_client_order_id=cid
   617	            )
   618	            self.db.save_order(order)
   619	        except Exception:
   620	            # Recovery can find a filled order by the durable client id.
   621	            self.db.log_event(
   622	                'ERROR',
   623	                'entry_request_failed',
   624	                'BUY request failed; recovery will reconcile by clientOrderId',
   625	                {'clientOrderId':cid}
   626	            )
   627	            raise
   628	
   629	        qty=float(order.get('executedQty',0))
   630	        spent=float(order.get('cummulativeQuoteQty',0))
   631	        avg=spent/qty if qty else 0
   632	
   633	        if qty<self._min_qty():
   634	            raise RuntimeError('BUY returned insufficient executed quantity')
   635	
   636	        self.db.state_delete('entry_client_order_id')
   637	        return order,qty,avg
   638	
   639	    def place_oco(self,qty,entry_price,trade_id=None):
   640	        qty=self.normalize_qty(qty)
   641	        if qty<=0:raise RuntimeError('Position quantity became zero after LOT_SIZE rounding')
   642	        tp=self.normalize_price(entry_price*(1+self.target_pct)); sl=self.normalize_price(entry_price*(1-self.stop_pct)); tick=float((self.filters.get('PRICE_FILTER') or {}).get('tickSize','0.01')); sl_limit=self.normalize_price(max(sl-tick*2,tick))
   643	        if not(tp>entry_price and sl<entry_price and sl_limit<sl):raise RuntimeError(f'Invalid TP/SL after tick rounding: entry={entry_price}, tp={tp}, sl={sl}, sl_limit={sl_limit}')
   644	        cid=f'WILLV4_OCO_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:20]}'; self._set_state('EXIT_PENDING')
   645	        result=self.client.create_oco_sell(self.symbol,self.client.decimal_format(qty),self.client.decimal_format(tp),self.client.decimal_format(sl),self.client.decimal_format(sl_limit),cid)
   646	        self.db.log_event('INFO','oco_created','Native TP/SL OCO created',result)
   647	        for leg in result.get('orderReports',[]):self.db.save_order(leg)
   648	        if trade_id is not None and result.get('orderListId') is not None:self.db.update_trade_oco(trade_id,result['orderListId'])
   649	        return result,tp,sl
   650	
   651	    def has_open_position(self):return self.db.open_trade(self.symbol) is not None and self._is_meaningful_position()
   652	    def _is_meaningful_position(self):return self.bot_base_balance()>=self._min_qty()
   653	
   654	
   655	    def _auto_scan_process(self):
   656	        """
   657	        Multi-symbol entry pipeline.
   658	
   659	        HARD SAFETY RULES:
   660	        - exactly one open position;
   661	        - RECONCILE_REQUIRED blocks all entries;
   662	        - scanner may only nominate STRICT_SIGNAL;
   663	        - HTF confirmation is mandatory when configured;
   664	        - selected symbol is revalidated after switching;
   665	        - Trader._risk_gate() remains the final risk barrier;
   666	        - DRY_RUN never reaches market_buy();
   667	        - any preparation/recovery error aborts the cycle.
   668	        """
   669	
   670	        # Full recovery is performed at startup, after symbol switches,
   671	        # and after ambiguous exchange operations. Do not run expensive
   672	        # REST reconciliation on every normal scanner cycle.
   673	        current_state = self.state()
   674	
   675	        if current_state != 'FLAT':
   676	            log.info(
   677	                'AUTO-SCAN BLOCKED: state=%s symbol=%s',
   678	                current_state,
   679	                self.symbol,
   680	            )
   681	            return
   682	
   683	        if self.db.open_trade() is not None:
   684	            log.warning(
   685	                'AUTO-SCAN BLOCKED: database reports an open trade'
   686	            )
   687	            return
   688	
   689	        # Never allow more than one managed position.
   690	        if self.max_open_positions != 1:
   691	            raise RuntimeError(
   692	                'Automatic scanner currently supports exactly one open position'
   693	            )
   694	
   695	        self.db.log_event(
   696	            'INFO',
   697	            'autoscan_start',
   698	            'Automatic multi-symbol scan started',
   699	            {
   700	                'symbols': self.auto_scan_symbols,
   701	                'interval': self.interval,
   702	            },
   703	        )
   704	
   705	        balance = self.available_quote()
   706	
   707	        if balance <= 0:
   708	            log.warning(
   709	                'AUTO-SCAN WAIT: no available quote balance'
   710	            )
   711	            return
   712	
   713	        controller = PortfolioController(
   714	            self.client,
   715	            balance_quote=balance,
   716	            symbols=self.auto_scan_symbols,
   717	        )
   718	
   719	        selection = controller.select(
   720	            has_open_position=False
   721	        )
   722	
   723	        if selection is None:
   724	            log.info(
   725	                'AUTO-SCAN: no STRICT_SIGNAL candidate passed risk checks'
   726	            )
   727	            self.db.log_event(
   728	                'INFO',
   729	                'autoscan_wait',
   730	                'No candidate passed strict signal and risk checks',
   731	            )
   732	            return
   733	
   734	        candidate = selection.candidate
   735	
   736	        self.db.log_event(
   737	            'INFO',
   738	            'autoscan_selection',
   739	            f'Selected candidate {candidate.symbol}',
   740	            {
   741	                'symbol': candidate.symbol,
   742	                'score': candidate.score,
   743	                'setup_state': candidate.setup_state,
   744	                'signal': candidate.signal,
   745	            },
   746	        )
   747	
   748	        # Controller must never be allowed to hand execution
   749	        # a weak/watch-only candidate.
   750	        if not bool(candidate.signal):
   751	            log.warning(
   752	                'AUTO-SCAN REJECTED non-strict candidate: %s',
   753	                candidate.symbol,
   754	            )
   755	            return
   756	
   757	        if self.require_htf_confirmation and not bool(
   758	            candidate.htf_confirmed
   759	        ):
   760	            log.warning(
   761	                'AUTO-SCAN REJECTED without HTF confirmation: %s',
   762	                candidate.symbol,
   763	            )
   764	            return
   765	
   766	        selected_symbol = candidate.symbol.upper()
   767	
   768	        log.info(
   769	            'AUTO-SCAN SELECTED %s score=%.2f setup=%.2f rr=%.2f atr=%.4f spread=%.4f',
   770	            selected_symbol,
   771	            candidate.score,
   772	            candidate.setup_score,
   773	            candidate.risk_reward,
   774	            candidate.atr_pct,
   775	            candidate.spread_pct,
   776	        )
   777	
   778	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   779	        # SWITCH SYMBOL
   780	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   781	
   782	        self.switch_symbol(selected_symbol)
   783	
   784	        if self.symbol != selected_symbol:
   785	            raise RuntimeError(
   786	                f'Symbol switch verification failed: '
   787	                f'{self.symbol} != {selected_symbol}'
   788	            )
   789	
   790	        if self.active_symbol != selected_symbol:
   791	            raise RuntimeError(
   792	                'active_symbol verification failed'
   793	            )
   794	
   795	        if self.state() != 'FLAT':
   796	            log.warning(
   797	                'AUTO-SCAN blocked after symbol switch: state=%s',
   798	                self.state(),
   799	            )
   800	            return
   801	
   802	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   803	        # PREPARE BASELINE + RECOVERY
   804	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   805	
   806	        self.ensure_foreign_base_balance_baseline()
   807	        self.recover_state()
   808	
   809	        if self.state() != 'FLAT':
   810	            log.warning(
   811	                'AUTO-SCAN blocked after recovery: state=%s',
   812	                self.state(),
   813	            )
   814	            return
   815	
   816	        if self.db.open_trade(self.symbol) is not None:
   817	            log.warning(
   818	                'AUTO-SCAN blocked: selected symbol has an open trade'
   819	            )
   820	            return
   821	
   822	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   823	        # FRESH REVALIDATION
   824	        #
   825	        # The first scanner result may already be stale because
   826	        # switch_symbol/recovery required REST requests.
   827	        # Re-scan ONLY the selected symbol before execution.
   828	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   829	
   830	        fresh_balance = self.available_quote()
   831	
   832	        fresh_controller = PortfolioController(
   833	            self.client,
   834	            balance_quote=fresh_balance,
   835	            symbols=[selected_symbol],
   836	        )
   837	
   838	        fresh_selection = fresh_controller.select(
   839	            has_open_position=False
   840	        )
   841	
   842	        if fresh_selection is None:
   843	            log.info(
   844	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: selected signal disappeared during revalidation'
   845	            )
   846	            return
   847	
   848	        fresh_candidate = fresh_selection.candidate
   849	
   850	        if not bool(fresh_candidate.signal):
   851	            log.warning(
   852	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: fresh candidate is not STRICT_SIGNAL'
   853	            )
   854	            return
   855	
   856	        if self.require_htf_confirmation and not bool(
   857	            fresh_candidate.htf_confirmed
   858	        ):
   859	            log.warning(
   860	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: fresh HTF confirmation failed'
   861	            )
   862	            return
   863	
   864	        if fresh_candidate.symbol.upper() != selected_symbol:
   865	            log.warning(
   866	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: selected symbol changed during revalidation'
   867	            )
   868	            return
   869	
   870	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   871	        # FINAL STRATEGY DATA
   872	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   873	
   874	        df = fetch_klines(
   875	            self.client,
   876	            self.symbol,
   877	            self.interval,
   878	            limit=250,
   879	        )
   880	
   881	        if len(df) < 100:
   882	            log.warning(
   883	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: insufficient candles for %s',
   884	                self.symbol,
   885	            )
   886	            return
   887	
   888	        closed = df.iloc[:-1].copy()
   889	
   890	        if len(closed) < 100:
   891	            log.warning(
   892	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: insufficient closed candles for %s',
   893	                self.symbol,
   894	            )
   895	            return
   896	
   897	        ind = calculate_indicators(
   898	            closed,
   899	            config_from_env(),
   900	        )
   901	
   902	        last = ind.iloc[-1]
   903	        last_time = str(ind.index[-1])
   904	
   905	        if not bool(last.get('long_signal', False)):
   906	            log.info(
   907	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: final strict signal disappeared for %s',
   908	                self.symbol,
   909	            )
   910	            return
   911	
   912	        # Per-symbol candle guard.
   913	        candle_key = f'last_signal_candle:{self.symbol}'
   914	
   915	        if self.db.state_get(candle_key) == last_time:
   916	            log.info(
   917	                'AUTO-SCAN WAIT: signal candle already processed: %s %s',
   918	                self.symbol,
   919	                last_time,
   920	            )
   921	            return
   922	
   923	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   924	        # FINAL TRADER RISK GATE
   925	        #
   926	        # This is deliberately kept even though RiskEngine already
   927	        # approved the candidate. Defense in depth.
   928	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   929	
   930	        allowed, reason = self._risk_gate(closed)
   931	
   932	        if not allowed:
   933	            self.db.log_event(
   934	                'INFO',
   935	                'risk_block',
   936	                reason,
   937	                {
   938	                    'symbol': self.symbol,
   939	                    'candle': last_time,
   940	                    'scanner_score': fresh_candidate.score,
   941	                },
   942	            )
   943	
   944	            self.db.state_set(
   945	                candle_key,
   946	                last_time,
   947	            )
   948	
   949	            log.info(
   950	                'AUTO-SCAN RISK BLOCK: %s',
   951	                reason,
   952	            )
   953	            return
   954	
   955	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   956	        # FINAL SAFETY SNAPSHOT
   957	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   958	
   959	        if self.state() != 'FLAT':
   960	            log.warning(
   961	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: state changed before entry'
   962	            )
   963	            return
   964	
   965	        if self.db.open_trade(self.symbol) is not None:
   966	            log.warning(
   967	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: open trade appeared before entry'
   968	            )
   969	            return
   970	
   971	        quote = self._position_quote(
   972	            float(last['close'])
   973	        )
   974	
   975	        if quote <= 0:
   976	            log.warning(
   977	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: calculated quote is zero'
   978	            )
   979	            return
   980	
   981	        if quote < self._min_notional():
   982	            log.warning(
   983	                'AUTO-SCAN ABORT: quote %.8f below minimum notional %.8f',
   984	                quote,
   985	                self._min_notional(),
   986	            )
   987	            return
   988	
   989	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   990	        # DRY RUN HARD STOP
   991	        # --------------------------------------------------------
   992	
   993	        if self.dry_run:
   994	            log.warning(
   995	                'DRY_RUN: would BUY %s quote=%.8f score=%.2f',
   996	                self.symbol,
   997	                quote,
   998	                fresh_candidate.score,
   999	            )
  1000	
  1001	            self.db.log_event(
  1002	                'INFO',
  1003	                'dry_run_entry',
  1004	                'Strict scanner signal passed all entry gates; no order created',
  1005	                {
  1006	                    'symbol': self.symbol,
  1007	                    'quote': quote,
  1008	                    'score': fresh_candidate.score,
  1009	                    'setup_score': fresh_candidate.setup_score,
  1010	                    'signal_strength': fresh_candidate.signal_strength,
  1011	                    'htf_confirmed': fresh_candidate.htf_confirmed,
  1012	                    'atr_pct': fresh_candidate.atr_pct,
  1013	                    'spread_pct': fresh_candidate.spread_pct,
  1014	                    'risk_reward': fresh_candidate.risk_reward,
  1015	                    'candle': last_time,
  1016	                },
  1017	            )
  1018	
  1019	            return
  1020	
  1021	        # --------------------------------------------------------
  1022	        # ACTUAL ENTRY
  1023	        #
  1024	        # setup() still blocks LIVE unless ALLOW_LIVE=true.
  1025	        # --------------------------------------------------------
  1026	
  1027	        order, qty, entry = self.market_buy(quote)
  1028	
  1029	        self.db.log_event(
  1030	            'INFO',
  1031	            'entry',
  1032	            'LONG market entry filled',
  1033	            order,
  1034	        )
  1035	
  1036	        self.db.save_trade(
  1037	            entry_time=utc_now(),
  1038	            symbol=self.symbol,
  1039	            side='LONG',
  1040	            entry_price=entry,
  1041	            quantity=qty,
  1042	            entry_order_id=str(order.get('orderId')),
  1043	            fees=0,
  1044	        )
  1045	
  1046	        trade = self.db.open_trade(self.symbol)
  1047	
  1048	        try:
  1049	            self.place_oco(
  1050	                qty,
  1051	                entry,
  1052	                trade_id=trade['id'] if trade else None,
  1053	            )
  1054	
  1055	        except Exception as e:
  1056	            self.db.log_event(
  1057	                'ERROR',
  1058	                'oco_failed_after_entry',
  1059	                str(e),
  1060	            )
  1061	
  1062	            self.notify(
  1063	                'WARNING\n'
  1064	                'BUY filled but OCO placement failed; '
  1065	                'trading is blocked until recovery.\n'
  1066	                f'{e}'
  1067	            )
  1068	
  1069	            raise
  1070	
  1071	        self._set_state('OPEN')
  1072	
  1073	        self.db.state_set(
  1074	            candle_key,
  1075	            last_time,
  1076	        )
  1077	
  1078	        self.notify(
  1079	            f'LONG ENTRY\n'
  1080	            f'{self.symbol}\n'
  1081	            f'qty={qty}\n'
  1082	            f'entry≈{entry:.8f}\n'
  1083	            f'Score={fresh_candidate.score:.2f}\n'
  1084	            f'Order={order.get("orderId")}'
  1085	        )
  1086	
  1087	    def process(self):
  1088	        if getattr(self, 'auto_scan_enabled', False):
  1089	            self._auto_scan_process()
  1090	            return
  1091	
  1092	        # Legacy single-symbol mode remains available by setting
  1093	        # AUTO_SCAN_ENABLED=false.
  1094	        self.recover_state()
  1095	
  1096	        if self.state() != 'FLAT':
  1097	            return
  1098	
  1099	        df = fetch_klines(
  1100	            self.client,
  1101	            self.symbol,
  1102	            self.interval,
  1103	            limit=250,
  1104	        )
  1105	
  1106	        if len(df) < 100:
  1107	            return
  1108	
  1109	        closed = df.iloc[:-1].copy()
  1110	
  1111	        ind = calculate_indicators(
  1112	            closed,
  1113	            config_from_env(),
  1114	        )
  1115	
  1116	        last = ind.iloc[-1]
  1117	        last_time = str(ind.index[-1])
  1118	
  1119	        candle_key = f'last_signal_candle:{self.symbol}'
  1120	
  1121	        if self.db.state_get(candle_key) == last_time:
  1122	            return
  1123	
  1124	        if not bool(last.get('long_signal', False)):
  1125	            self.db.state_set(
  1126	                candle_key,
  1127	                last_time,
  1128	            )
  1129	            return
  1130	
  1131	        allowed, reason = self._risk_gate(closed)
  1132	
  1133	        if not allowed:
  1134	            self.db.log_event(
  1135	                'INFO',
  1136	                'risk_block',
  1137	                reason,
  1138	                {
  1139	                    'symbol': self.symbol,
  1140	                    'candle': last_time,
  1141	                },
  1142	            )
  1143	
  1144	            self.db.state_set(
  1145	                candle_key,
  1146	                last_time,
  1147	            )
  1148	
  1149	            return
  1150	
  1151	        quote = self._position_quote(
  1152	            float(last['close'])
  1153	        )
  1154	
  1155	        if self.dry_run:
  1156	            log.warning(
  1157	                'DRY_RUN legacy mode: would BUY %s quote=%.8f',
  1158	                self.symbol,
  1159	                quote,
  1160	            )
  1161	            return
  1162	
  1163	        order, qty, entry = self.market_buy(quote)
  1164	
  1165	        self.db.log_event(
  1166	            'INFO',
  1167	            'entry',
  1168	            'LONG market entry filled',
  1169	            order,
  1170	        )
  1171	
  1172	        self.db.save_trade(
  1173	            entry_time=utc_now(),
  1174	            symbol=self.symbol,
  1175	            side='LONG',
  1176	            entry_price=entry,
  1177	            quantity=qty,
  1178	            entry_order_id=str(order.get('orderId')),
  1179	            fees=0,
  1180	        )
  1181	
  1182	        trade = self.db.open_trade(self.symbol)
  1183	
  1184	        try:
  1185	            self.place_oco(
  1186	                qty,
  1187	                entry,
  1188	                trade_id=trade['id'] if trade else None,
  1189	            )
  1190	
  1191	        except Exception as e:
  1192	            self.db.log_event(
  1193	                'ERROR',
  1194	                'oco_failed_after_entry',
  1195	                str(e),
  1196	            )
  1197	
  1198	            self.notify(
  1199	                'WARNING\n'
  1200	                'BUY filled but OCO placement failed; '
  1201	                'trading is blocked until recovery.\n'
  1202	                f'{e}'
  1203	            )
  1204	
  1205	            raise
  1206	
  1207	        self._set_state('OPEN')
  1208	
  1209	        self.db.state_set(
  1210	            candle_key,
  1211	            last_time,
  1212	        )
  1213	
  1214	        self.notify(
  1215	            f'LONG ENTRY\n'
  1216	            f'{self.symbol}\n'
  1217	            f'qty={qty}\n'
  1218	            f'entry≈{entry:.8f}\n'
  1219	            f'Order={order.get("orderId")}'
  1220	        )
  1221	
  1222	    def run(self):
  1223	        self.setup()
  1224	        while True:
  1225	            try:self.process()
  1226	            except Exception as e:self.db.log_event('ERROR','loop_error',str(e)); self.notify(f'Williams ERROR\n{self.symbol}\n{type(e).__name__}: {e}')
  1227	            time.sleep(self.poll_seconds)
  1228	
  1229	if __name__=='__main__':Trader().run()

############################
# 21. STRATEGY
############################
     1	import os
     2	import numpy as np
     3	import pandas as pd
     4	
     5	
     6	def smma(series, period):
     7	    out = pd.Series(index=series.index, dtype=float)
     8	
     9	    if len(series) < period:
    10	        return out
    11	
    12	    out.iloc[period - 1] = series.iloc[:period].mean()
    13	
    14	    for i in range(period, len(series)):
    15	        out.iloc[i] = (
    16	            (out.iloc[i - 1] * (period - 1) + series.iloc[i]) / period
    17	        )
    18	
    19	    return out
    20	
    21	
    22	def calculate_indicators(df, cfg):
    23	    x = df.copy()
    24	
    25	    median = (x["high"] + x["low"]) / 2
    26	
    27	    # Williams Alligator
    28	    x["jaw"] = smma(median, cfg["jaw"])
    29	    x["teeth"] = smma(median, cfg["teeth"])
    30	    x["lips"] = smma(median, cfg["lips"])
    31	
    32	    x["jaw_shifted"] = x["jaw"].shift(cfg["jaw_shift"])
    33	    x["teeth_shifted"] = x["teeth"].shift(cfg["teeth_shift"])
    34	    x["lips_shifted"] = x["lips"].shift(cfg["lips_shift"])
    35	
    36	    # Awesome Oscillator
    37	    x["ao"] = (
    38	        median.rolling(cfg["ao_fast"]).mean()
    39	        - median.rolling(cfg["ao_slow"]).mean()
    40	    )
    41	
    42	    # Accelerator Oscillator
    43	    x["ac"] = x["ao"] - x["ao"].rolling(cfg["ac_period"]).mean()
    44	
    45	    # Fractals
    46	    left = cfg["fractal_left"]
    47	    right = cfg["fractal_right"]
    48	
    49	    x["fractal_up"] = False
    50	    x["fractal_down"] = False
    51	
    52	    for i in range(left, len(x) - right):
    53	        if (
    54	            x["high"].iloc[i] > x["high"].iloc[i-left:i].max()
    55	            and x["high"].iloc[i] > x["high"].iloc[i+1:i+right+1].max()
    56	        ):
    57	            x.iloc[i, x.columns.get_loc("fractal_up")] = True
    58	
    59	        if (
    60	            x["low"].iloc[i] < x["low"].iloc[i-left:i].min()
    61	            and x["low"].iloc[i] < x["low"].iloc[i+1:i+right+1].min()
    62	        ):
    63	            x.iloc[i, x.columns.get_loc("fractal_down")] = True
    64	
    65	    x["confirmed_up_level"] = np.nan
    66	    x["confirmed_down_level"] = np.nan
    67	
    68	    for i in range(left + right, len(x)):
    69	        fi = i - right
    70	
    71	        if bool(x["fractal_up"].iloc[fi]):
    72	            x.iloc[
    73	                i,
    74	                x.columns.get_loc("confirmed_up_level"),
    75	            ] = x["high"].iloc[fi]
    76	
    77	        if bool(x["fractal_down"].iloc[fi]):
    78	            x.iloc[
    79	                i,
    80	                x.columns.get_loc("confirmed_down_level"),
    81	            ] = x["low"].iloc[fi]
    82	
    83	    x["last_up_level"] = x["confirmed_up_level"].ffill()
    84	    x["last_down_level"] = x["confirmed_down_level"].ffill()
    85	
    86	    # Alligator direction
    87	    x["bullish_alligator"] = (
    88	        (x["lips_shifted"] > x["teeth_shifted"])
    89	        & (x["teeth_shifted"] > x["jaw_shifted"])
    90	        & (x["close"] > x["lips_shifted"])
    91	    )
    92	
    93	    x["bearish_alligator"] = (
    94	        (x["lips_shifted"] < x["teeth_shifted"])
    95	        & (x["teeth_shifted"] < x["jaw_shifted"])
    96	        & (x["close"] < x["lips_shifted"])
    97	    )
    98	
    99	    # Alligator spread / awake state
   100	    spread = (
   101	        x[
   102	            ["jaw_shifted", "teeth_shifted", "lips_shifted"]
   103	        ].max(axis=1)
   104	        - x[
   105	            ["jaw_shifted", "teeth_shifted", "lips_shifted"]
   106	        ].min(axis=1)
   107	    ) / x["close"].replace(0, np.nan)
   108	
   109	    x["alligator_spread_pct"] = spread
   110	    x["alligator_awake"] = (
   111	        spread >= cfg["min_alligator_spread_pct"]
   112	    )
   113	
   114	    # Individual long conditions.
   115	    # These are intentionally separate so the scanner can understand
   116	    # why a setup is close to a valid signal.
   117	    x["long_bullish"] = x["bullish_alligator"]
   118	    x["long_awake"] = x["alligator_awake"]
   119	    x["long_ao_positive"] = x["ao"] > 0
   120	    x["long_ac_positive"] = x["ac"] > 0
   121	    x["long_fractal_ready"] = x["last_up_level"].notna()
   122	    x["long_above_fractal"] = (
   123	        x["last_up_level"].notna()
   124	        & (x["close"] > x["last_up_level"])
   125	    )
   126	    x["long_previous_below_fractal"] = (
   127	        x["last_up_level"].notna()
   128	        & x["last_up_level"].shift(1).notna()
   129	        & (
   130	            x["close"].shift(1)
   131	            <= x["last_up_level"].shift(1)
   132	        )
   133	    )
   134	
   135	    # Strict entry signal.
   136	    #
   137	    # IMPORTANT:
   138	    # This remains the actual BUY signal.
   139	    # We are NOT weakening it.
   140	    x["long_signal"] = (
   141	        x["long_bullish"]
   142	        & x["long_awake"]
   143	        & x["long_ao_positive"]
   144	        & x["long_ac_positive"]
   145	        & x["long_fractal_ready"]
   146	        & x["long_above_fractal"]
   147	        & x["long_previous_below_fractal"]
   148	    )
   149	
   150	    # Short-side diagnostics.
   151	    x["short_bearish"] = x["bearish_alligator"]
   152	    x["short_awake"] = x["alligator_awake"]
   153	    x["short_ao_negative"] = x["ao"] < 0
   154	    x["short_ac_negative"] = x["ac"] < 0
   155	    x["short_fractal_ready"] = x["last_down_level"].notna()
   156	    x["short_below_fractal"] = (
   157	        x["last_down_level"].notna()
   158	        & (x["close"] < x["last_down_level"])
   159	    )
   160	    x["short_previous_above_fractal"] = (
   161	        x["last_down_level"].notna()
   162	        & x["last_down_level"].shift(1).notna()
   163	        & (
   164	            x["close"].shift(1)
   165	            >= x["last_down_level"].shift(1)
   166	        )
   167	    )
   168	
   169	    x["short_signal"] = (
   170	        x["short_bearish"]
   171	        & x["short_awake"]
   172	        & x["short_ao_negative"]
   173	        & x["short_ac_negative"]
   174	        & x["short_fractal_ready"]
   175	        & x["short_below_fractal"]
   176	        & x["short_previous_above_fractal"]
   177	    )
   178	
   179	    # Long setup strength.
   180	    #
   181	    # This is NOT an entry signal.
   182	    # It is a normalized measure used only for ranking markets.
   183	    long_components = [
   184	        "long_bullish",
   185	        "long_awake",
   186	        "long_ao_positive",
   187	        "long_ac_positive",
   188	        "long_fractal_ready",
   189	        "long_above_fractal",
   190	        "long_previous_below_fractal",
   191	    ]
   192	
   193	    x["long_setup_score"] = (
   194	        x[long_components]
   195	        .astype(int)
   196	        .sum(axis=1)
   197	        / len(long_components)
   198	        * 100.0
   199	    )
   200	
   201	    # Distance to the confirmed breakout level.
   202	    x["long_breakout_distance_pct"] = np.where(
   203	        x["last_up_level"].notna()
   204	        & (x["last_up_level"] > 0),
   205	        (x["close"] / x["last_up_level"] - 1.0) * 100.0,
   206	        np.nan,
   207	    )
   208	
   209	    return x
   210	
   211	
   212	def config_from_env(env=os.environ):
   213	    return {
   214	        "jaw": int(env.get("ALLIGATOR_JAW", "13")),
   215	        "teeth": int(env.get("ALLIGATOR_TEETH", "8")),
   216	        "lips": int(env.get("ALLIGATOR_LIPS", "5")),
   217	        "jaw_shift": int(env.get("JAW_SHIFT", "8")),
   218	        "teeth_shift": int(env.get("TEETH_SHIFT", "5")),
   219	        "lips_shift": int(env.get("LIPS_SHIFT", "3")),
   220	        "ao_fast": int(env.get("AO_FAST", "5")),
   221	        "ao_slow": int(env.get("AO_SLOW", "34")),
   222	        "ac_period": int(env.get("AC_PERIOD", "5")),
   223	        "fractal_left": int(env.get("FRACTAL_LEFT", "2")),
   224	        "fractal_right": int(env.get("FRACTAL_RIGHT", "2")),
   225	        "min_alligator_spread_pct": float(
   226	            env.get("MIN_ALLIGATOR_SPREAD_PCT", "0.001")
   227	        ),
   228	    }

     1	import asyncio,json,logging,os,threading,time
     2	from datetime import datetime,timezone
     3	import websocket
     4	import pandas as pd
     5	from binance_client import BinanceSpotClient
     6	from db import Database
