     8	from strategy import calculate_indicators,config_from_env
     9	log=logging.getLogger('williams-ws')
    10	class WebSocketHub:
    11	    def __init__(self):
    12	        self.symbol=os.getenv('SYMBOL','BTCUSDT').upper(); self.interval=os.getenv('INTERVAL','1h'); self.testnet=os.getenv('TESTNET','true').lower()=='true'; self.api_key=os.getenv('BINANCE_API_KEY',''); self.api_secret=os.getenv('BINANCE_API_SECRET',''); self.db=Database(
    13	            os.getenv('WILLIAMS_DB_PATH')
    14	            or os.getenv('DB_PATH')
    15	            or 'data/trader.sqlite3'
    16	        ); self.client=BinanceSpotClient(self.api_key,self.api_secret,self.testnet); self.clients=set(); self.lock=threading.RLock(); self.stop_event=threading.Event(); self.threads=[]; self.price=None; self.candles=[]; self.position=None; self.orders=[]; self.tp=None; self.sl=None; self.balance=None; self.base_asset=None; self.quote_asset=None; self.last_error=None; self.market_connected=False; self.user_connected=False; self._market_ws=None; self._user_ws=None; self.user_subscription_id=None; self.started=False
    17	    def configure_credentials(self,key,secret,testnet=True):
    18	        with self.lock:
    19	            self.api_key=key.strip()
    20	            self.api_secret=secret.strip()
    21	            self.testnet=bool(testnet)
    22	            self.client=BinanceSpotClient(
    23	                self.api_key,
    24	                self.api_secret,
    25	                self.testnet
    26	            )
    27	            self.user_connected=False
    28	            self.user_subscription_id=None
    29	
    30	        # Only start the user thread when the hub is already running.
    31	        # During startup(), start() will create both threads itself.
    32	        if self.started and not self.stop_event.is_set():
    33	            if not any(
    34	                t.name=='williams-user-ws' and t.is_alive()
    35	                for t in self.threads
    36	            ):
    37	                t=threading.Thread(
    38	                    target=self._user_loop,
    39	                    daemon=True,
    40	                    name='williams-user-ws'
    41	                )
    42	                t.start()
    43	                self.threads.append(t)
    44	
    45	    def add_client(self,c):
    46	        with self.lock:self.clients.add(c); snap=self.snapshot()
    47	        self._queue(c,{'type':'snapshot','data':snap})
    48	    def remove_client(self,c):
    49	        with self.lock:self.clients.discard(c)
    50	    def _queue(self,c,p):
    51	        try:c.loop.call_soon_threadsafe(c.queue.put_nowait,p)
    52	        except Exception:pass
    53	    def broadcast(self,p):
    54	        with self.lock:cs=list(self.clients)
    55	        for c in cs:self._queue(c,p)
    56	    def snapshot(self):
    57	        with self.lock:
    58	            return {
    59	                'symbol':self.symbol,
    60	                'interval':self.interval,
    61	                'testnet':self.testnet,
    62	                'price':self.price,
    63	                'quote_balance':self.balance,
    64	                'position':dict(self.position) if self.position else None,
    65	                'take_profit_price':self.tp,
    66	                'stop_loss_price':self.sl,
    67	                'candles':list(self.candles),
    68	                'orders':list(self.orders),
    69	                'server_time':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    70	                'market_connected':self.market_connected,
    71	                'user_connected':self.user_connected,
    72	                'user_subscription_id':self.user_subscription_id,
    73	                'ws_connected':(
    74	                    self.market_connected
    75	                    and (not self.api_key or self.user_connected)
    76	                ),
    77	                'last_error':self.last_error,
    78	            }
    79	
    80	    def start(self):
    81	        self.stop_event.clear()
    82	        self.started=True
    83	
    84	        # Bootstrap only once per running session.
    85	        self._bootstrap()
    86	
    87	        if not any(
    88	            t.name=='williams-market-ws' and t.is_alive()
    89	            for t in self.threads
    90	        ):
    91	            t=threading.Thread(
    92	                target=self._market_loop,
    93	                daemon=True,
    94	                name='williams-market-ws'
    95	            )
    96	            t.start()
    97	            self.threads.append(t)
    98	
    99	        if not any(
   100	            t.name=='williams-user-ws' and t.is_alive()
   101	            for t in self.threads
   102	        ):
   103	            t=threading.Thread(
   104	                target=self._user_loop,
   105	                daemon=True,
   106	                name='williams-user-ws'
   107	            )
   108	            t.start()
   109	            self.threads.append(t)
   110	
   111	    def stop(self):
   112	        self.started=False
   113	        self.stop_event.set()
   114	
   115	        for ws in (self._market_ws,self._user_ws):
   116	            if ws is not None:
   117	                try:
   118	                    ws.close()
   119	                except Exception:
   120	                    pass
   121	
   122	        self.market_connected=False
   123	        self.user_connected=False
   124	        self.user_subscription_id=None
   125	
   126	        current=threading.current_thread()
   127	
   128	        for t in list(self.threads):
   129	            if t is current:
   130	                continue
   131	            try:
   132	                t.join(timeout=3)
   133	            except Exception:
   134	                pass
   135	
   136	        self.threads=[
   137	            t for t in self.threads
   138	            if t.is_alive() and t is not current
   139	        ]
   140	
   141	        self._market_ws=None
   142	        self._user_ws=None
   143	
   144	    def _bootstrap(self):
   145	        try:
   146	            self.client.sync_time(); info=self.client.exchange_info(self.symbol); s=info['symbols'][0]; self.base_asset=s['baseAsset'];self.quote_asset=s['quoteAsset']; self._load_candles();self._refresh_account()
   147	        except Exception as e:self.last_error=f'bootstrap: {e}'
   148	    def _load_candles(self):
   149	        df=fetch_klines(self.client,self.symbol,self.interval,limit=120)
   150	        if df.empty:return
   151	        ind=calculate_indicators(df.iloc[:-1].copy(),config_from_env()); self.candles=[self._row(i,r) for i,r in ind.iterrows()]
   152	    def _row(self,i,r):return {'time':i.isoformat(),'open':float(r.open),'high':float(r.high),'low':float(r.low),'close':float(r.close),'jaw':None if pd.isna(r.jaw_shifted) else float(r.jaw_shifted),'teeth':None if pd.isna(r.teeth_shifted) else float(r.teeth_shifted),'lips':None if pd.isna(r.lips_shifted) else float(r.lips_shifted),'ao':None if pd.isna(r.ao) else float(r.ao),'long_signal':bool(r.long_signal),'fractal_up':bool(r.fractal_up),'fractal_down':bool(r.fractal_down)}
   153	    def _refresh_account(self):
   154	        if not self.api_key or not self.api_secret:return
   155	        try:
   156	            a=self.client.account(); self.balance=next((float(b['free']) for b in a.get('balances',[]) if b['asset']==self.quote_asset),0.0); qty=next((float(b['free'])+float(b['locked']) for b in a.get('balances',[]) if b['asset']==self.base_asset),0.0); tr=self.db.open_trade(); self.position={'side':'LONG','quantity':qty,'entry_price':float(tr['entry_price']) if tr else None} if tr and qty>0 else None; self._refresh_orders()
   157	        except Exception as e:self.last_error=f'account: {e}'
   158	    def _refresh_orders(self):
   159	        try:
   160	            active=[o for o in self.db.recent_orders(self.symbol,100) if str(o.get('status','')).upper() in {'NEW','PENDING_NEW','PARTIALLY_FILLED'}];self.orders=active[:20];self.tp=self.sl=None
   161	            for o in active:
   162	                typ=str(o.get('type','')).upper()
   163	                if 'TAKE_PROFIT' in typ and o.get('price'):self.tp=float(o['price'])
   164	                elif 'STOP_LOSS' in typ:self.sl=float(o.get('stop_price') or o.get('price') or 0) or None
   165	        except Exception:pass
   166	    def _market_url(self):
   167	        base='wss://stream.testnet.binance.vision' if self.testnet else 'wss://stream.binance.com:9443';s=self.symbol.lower();return f'{base}/stream?streams={s}@aggTrade/{s}@kline_{self.interval}'
   168	    def _market_loop(self):
   169	        delay=1
   170	
   171	        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
   172	            app=None
   173	
   174	            try:
   175	                app=websocket.WebSocketApp(
   176	                    self._market_url(),
   177	                    on_open=lambda ws:self._set_market(True),
   178	                    on_message=self._on_market_message,
   179	                    on_error=lambda ws,e:self._set_market(
   180	                        False,
   181	                        str(e)
   182	                    ),
   183	                    on_close=lambda ws,c,m:self._set_market(
   184	                        False,
   185	                        f'closed {c} {m}'
   186	                    )
   187	                )
   188	
   189	                self._market_ws=app
   190	
   191	                app.run_forever(
   192	                    ping_interval=30,
   193	                    ping_timeout=20
   194	                )
   195	
   196	                delay=1
   197	
   198	            except Exception as e:
   199	                self._set_market(False,str(e))
   200	
   201	            finally:
   202	                if self._market_ws is app:
   203	                    self._market_ws=None
   204	
   205	            if not self.stop_event.is_set():
   206	                self.stop_event.wait(delay)
   207	                delay=min(delay*2,30)
   208	
   209	    def _set_market(self,connected,error=None):
   210	        self.market_connected=connected
   211	        if error:self.last_error=f'market ws: {error}'
   212	        self.broadcast({'type':'ticker','data':self._live()})
   213	    def _user_loop(self):
   214	        delay=1
   215	
   216	        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
   217	            if not self.api_key or not self.api_secret:
   218	                self.user_connected=False
   219	                self.stop_event.wait(1)
   220	                continue
   221	
   222	            app=None
   223	
   224	            try:
   225	                # Synchronise local timestamp with Binance before signing.
   226	                try:
   227	                    self.client.sync_time()
   228	                except Exception as e:
   229	                    self._set_user(
   230	                        False,
   231	                        f"sync_time: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
   232	                    )
   233	                    self.stop_event.wait(delay)
   234	                    continue
   235	
   236	                params=self.client.websocket_signature_params()
   237	
   238	                url=(
   239	                    'wss://ws-api.testnet.binance.vision/ws-api/v3'
   240	                    if self.testnet
   241	                    else
   242	                    'wss://ws-api.binance.com:443/ws-api/v3'
   243	                )
   244	
   245	                self.user_connected=False
   246	                self.user_subscription_id=None
   247	
   248	                def opened(ws):
   249	                    self._user_ws=ws
   250	
   251	                    request={
   252	                        'id':f'williams-user-{int(time.time()*1000)}',
   253	                        'method':'userDataStream.subscribe.signature',
   254	                        'params':params,
   255	                    }
   256	
   257	                    ws.send(
   258	                        json.dumps(
   259	                            request,
   260	                            separators=(',',':')
   261	                        )
   262	                    )
   263	
   264	                def on_message(ws,raw):
   265	                    self._on_user_message(ws,raw)
   266	
   267	                def on_error(ws,error):
   268	                    self._set_user(
   269	                        False,
   270	                        f'{type(error).__name__}: {error}'
   271	                    )
   272	
   273	                def on_close(ws,code,msg):
   274	                    self.user_connected=False
   275	                    self.user_subscription_id=None
   276	
   277	                    if not self.stop_event.is_set():
   278	                        self.last_error=(
   279	                            f'user ws: closed {code} {msg}'
   280	                        )
   281	
   282	                    self.broadcast({
   283	                        'type':'account',
   284	                        'data':self._live()
   285	                    })
   286	
   287	                # Binance requires the API key in the HTTP/WebSocket header.
   288	                headers=[
   289	                    f'X-MBX-APIKEY: {self.api_key}'
   290	                ]
   291	
   292	                app=websocket.WebSocketApp(
   293	                    url,
   294	                    header=headers,
   295	                    on_open=opened,
   296	                    on_message=on_message,
   297	                    on_error=on_error,
   298	                    on_close=on_close
   299	                )
   300	
   301	                self._user_ws=app
   302	
   303	                app.run_forever(
   304	                    ping_interval=30,
   305	                    ping_timeout=20
   306	                )
   307	
   308	            except Exception as e:
   309	                self._set_user(
   310	                    False,
   311	                    f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
   312	                )
   313	
   314	            finally:
   315	                self.user_connected=False
   316	
   317	                if self._user_ws is app:
   318	                    self._user_ws=None
   319	
   320	            if self.stop_event.is_set():
   321	                break
   322	
   323	            self.stop_event.wait(delay)
   324	            delay=min(delay*2,30)
   325	
   326	    def _set_user(self,connected,error=None):
   327	        self.user_connected=connected
   328	
   329	        if not connected:
   330	            self.user_subscription_id=None
   331	
   332	        if error:
   333	            self.last_error=f'user ws: {error}'
   334	
   335	        self.broadcast({
   336	            'type':'account',
   337	            'data':self._live()
   338	        })
   339	
   340	    def _on_market_message(self,ws,raw):
   341	        try:
   342	            d=json.loads(raw).get('data',{}); ev=d.get('e')
   343	            if ev=='aggTrade':self.price=float(d['p']);self.broadcast({'type':'ticker','data':self._live()})
   344	            elif ev=='kline':self._update_kline(d['k'])
   345	        except Exception as e:log.debug('market parse: %s',e)
   346	    def _update_kline(self,k):
   347	        item={'time':datetime.fromtimestamp(int(k['t'])/1000,tz=timezone.utc).isoformat(),'open':float(k['o']),'high':float(k['h']),'low':float(k['l']),'close':float(k['c'])}
   348	        raw=list(self.candles)
   349	        if raw and raw[-1].get('time')==item['time']:raw[-1].update(item)
   350	        else:raw.append(item)
   351	        raw=raw[-120:];df=pd.DataFrame(raw);df['time']=pd.to_datetime(df['time'],utc=True);df=df.set_index('time')[['open','high','low','close']];ind=calculate_indicators(df,config_from_env());self.candles=[self._row(i,r) for i,r in ind.iterrows()][-120:];self.broadcast({'type':'candle','data':self.candles[-1]})
   352	    def _on_user_message(self,ws,raw):
   353	        try:
   354	            msg=json.loads(raw)
   355	
   356	            # Successful signed user-stream subscription.
   357	            if (
   358	                msg.get('status')==200
   359	                and isinstance(msg.get('result'),dict)
   360	                and msg.get('result',{}).get('subscriptionId') is not None
   361	            ):
   362	                self.user_subscription_id=(
   363	                    msg['result']['subscriptionId']
   364	                )
   365	                self.last_error=None
   366	                self._set_user(True)
   367	                return
   368	
   369	            # WS API error.
   370	            if msg.get('status') not in (None,200):
   371	                err=msg.get('error') or {}
   372	
   373	                if isinstance(err,dict):
   374	                    code=err.get('code')
   375	                    text=err.get('msg')
   376	                else:
   377	                    code=None
   378	                    text=str(err)
   379	
   380	                self._set_user(
   381	                    False,
   382	                    f'Binance WS API error code={code}: {text}'
   383	                )
   384	                return
   385	
   386	            event=msg.get('event',msg)
   387	
   388	            if not isinstance(event,dict):
   389	                return
   390	
   391	            event_type=event.get('e')
   392	
   393	            if event_type=='eventStreamTerminated':
   394	                self._set_user(
   395	                    False,
   396	                    'Binance user stream terminated'
   397	                )
   398	
   399	                try:
   400	                    ws.close()
   401	                except Exception:
   402	                    pass
   403	
   404	                return
   405	
   406	            if event_type=='executionReport':
   407	                o={
   408	                    'symbol':event.get('s'),
   409	                    'side':event.get('S'),
   410	                    'type':event.get('o'),
   411	                    'orderId':event.get('i'),
   412	                    'orderListId':event.get('g'),
   413	                    'clientOrderId':event.get('c'),
   414	                    'status':event.get('X'),
   415	                    'price':event.get('p'),
   416	                    'stopPrice':event.get('P'),
   417	                    'origQty':event.get('q'),
   418	                    'executedQty':event.get('z'),
   419	                    'cummulativeQuoteQty':event.get('Z'),
   420	                    'time':event.get('O'),
   421	                    'updateTime':event.get('T'),
   422	                }
   423	
   424	                if o['symbol']==self.symbol:
   425	                    self.db.save_order(o)
   426	                    self._refresh_orders()
   427	
   428	                    if str(o['status']).upper()=='FILLED':
   429	                        self._refresh_account()
   430	
   431	            elif event_type=='outboundAccountPosition':
   432	                self._refresh_account()
   433	
   434	            elif event_type=='balanceUpdate':
   435	                self._refresh_account()
   436	
   437	            self.broadcast({
   438	                'type':'account',
   439	                'data':self._live()
   440	            })
   441	
   442	        except Exception as e:
   443	            log.debug(
   444	                'user parse: %s',
   445	                e
   446	            )
   447	
   448	    def _live(self):
   449	        with self.lock:p=self.price;pos=dict(self.position) if self.position else None;tp=self.tp;sl=self.sl;bal=self.balance
   450	        pnl=pct=None
   451	        if pos and p is not None and pos.get('entry_price'):pnl=(p-pos['entry_price'])*pos['quantity'];pct=p/pos['entry_price']-1
   452	        return {'symbol':self.symbol,'interval':self.interval,'testnet':self.testnet,'price':p,'quote_balance':bal,'position':pos,'take_profit_price':tp,'stop_loss_price':sl,'pnl':pnl,'pnl_pct':pct,'server_time':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'ws_connected':self.market_connected and (not self.api_key or self.user_connected),'last_error':self.last_error}

############################
# 23. DATA
############################
     1	import time, pandas as pd, requests
     2	BASE_URL='https://testnet.binance.vision'
     3	
     4	def _frame(rows):
     5	    cols=['open_time','open','high','low','close','volume','close_time','quote_volume','trades','taker_buy_base','taker_buy_quote','ignore']
     6	    df=pd.DataFrame(rows,columns=cols)
     7	    if df.empty:return df
     8	    for c in ['open','high','low','close','volume']:df[c]=pd.to_numeric(df[c],errors='coerce')
     9	    df['open_time']=pd.to_datetime(df['open_time'],unit='ms',utc=True);df['close_time']=pd.to_datetime(df['close_time'],unit='ms',utc=True)
    10	    return df.set_index('open_time')[['open','high','low','close','volume','close_time']].dropna()
    11	
    12	def fetch_klines(client_or_symbol, symbol_or_interval, interval=None, limit=200, start=None, end=None):
    13	    """Live: fetch_klines(client, symbol, interval, limit=200). Historical: fetch_klines(symbol, interval, start, end)."""
    14	    if hasattr(client_or_symbol,'klines'):
    15	        client=client_or_symbol;symbol=symbol_or_interval;iv=interval
    16	        return _frame(client.klines(symbol,iv,limit=limit))
    17	    symbol=client_or_symbol;iv=symbol_or_interval
    18	    # Backward-compatible positional form: fetch_klines(symbol, interval, start, end).
    19	    if interval is not None and isinstance(interval,(str,pd.Timestamp)) and (limit is not None) and isinstance(limit,(str,pd.Timestamp)) and start is None and end is None:
    20	        start,end=interval,limit
    21	    elif start is None:
    22	        start=interval
    23	    params={'symbol':symbol,'interval':iv,'limit':1000}
    24	    start_ms=int(pd.Timestamp(start,tz='UTC').timestamp()*1000) if start else None
    25	    end_ms=int(pd.Timestamp(end,tz='UTC').timestamp()*1000) if end else None
    26	    if start_ms is not None:params['startTime']=start_ms
    27	    if end_ms is not None:params['endTime']=end_ms
    28	    rows=[];cursor=start_ms
    29	    while True:
    30	        p=dict(params)
    31	        if cursor is not None:p['startTime']=cursor
    32	        r=requests.get(BASE_URL+'/api/v3/klines',params=p,timeout=20);r.raise_for_status();batch=r.json()
    33	        if not batch:break
    34	        rows.extend(batch)
    35	        if len(batch)<1000:break
    36	        cursor=int(batch[-1][0])+1;time.sleep(0.05)
    37	        if end_ms is not None and cursor>=end_ms:break
    38	    return _frame(rows)
    39	
    40	def save_csv(df,path):df.reset_index().to_csv(path,index=False)

# 24. DATABASE
############################
     1	import os
     2	import json, sqlite3
     3	from pathlib import Path
     4	from datetime import datetime, timezone
     5	from contextlib import contextmanager
     6	
     7	class Database:
     8	    SCHEMA_VERSION = 2
     9	    def __init__(self, path=None):
    10	        path = path or os.getenv('WILLIAMS_DB_PATH') or 'data/trader.sqlite3'
    11	        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    12	        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False, timeout=20)
    13	        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    14	        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=20000')
    15	        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    16	        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL')
    17	        self._transaction_active = False
    18	        self.init()
    19	
    20	    @contextmanager
    21	    def transaction(self):
    22	        """
    23	        Atomic transaction for multi-step DB operations.
    24	
    25	        While active, state_set/state_delete/log_event do not commit
    26	        individually. The whole operation is committed together or
    27	        rolled back together on exception.
    28	        """
    29	        if self._transaction_active:
    30	            yield self
    31	            return
    32	
    33	        self._transaction_active = True
    34	        try:
    35	            self.conn.execute('BEGIN')
    36	            yield self
    37	            self.conn.commit()
    38	        except Exception:
    39	            self.conn.rollback()
    40	            raise
    41	        finally:
    42	            self._transaction_active = False
    43	
    44	    def init(self):
    45	        self.conn.executescript('''
    46	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bot_state(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,value TEXT NOT NULL);
    47	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candles(symbol TEXT NOT NULL,interval TEXT NOT NULL,open_time TEXT NOT NULL,open REAL,high REAL,low REAL,close REAL,volume REAL,PRIMARY KEY(symbol,interval,open_time));
    48	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,symbol TEXT,side TEXT,type TEXT,order_id TEXT,order_list_id TEXT,client_order_id TEXT,status TEXT,price REAL,stop_price REAL,quantity REAL,raw_json TEXT);
    49	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,entry_time TEXT,exit_time TEXT,symbol TEXT,side TEXT,entry_price REAL,exit_price REAL,quantity REAL,pnl REAL,pnl_pct REAL,reason TEXT,entry_order_id TEXT,exit_order_list_id TEXT,fees REAL DEFAULT 0);
    50	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,level TEXT,event TEXT,message TEXT,raw_json TEXT);
    51	        ''')
    52	        self.state_set('schema_version', self.SCHEMA_VERSION)
    53	        self.state_set('position_state', self.state_get('position_state', 'FLAT'))
    54	        self.conn.commit()
    55	
    56	    def state_get(self, key, default=None):
    57	        r = self.conn.execute('SELECT value FROM bot_state WHERE key=?', (key,)).fetchone()
    58	        return default if r is None else r['value']
    59	
    60	    def state_set(self, key, value):
    61	        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO bot_state(key,value) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value', (key, str(value)))
    62	        if not self._transaction_active:
    63	            self.conn.commit()
    64	
    65	    def state_delete(self, key):
    66	        self.conn.execute('DELETE FROM bot_state WHERE key=?', (key,))
    67	        if not self._transaction_active:
    68	            self.conn.commit()
    69	
    70	    def log_event(self, level, event, message, raw=None):
    71	        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO events(level,event,message,raw_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?)', (level, event, message, json.dumps(raw, default=str) if raw is not None else None))
    72	        if not self._transaction_active:
    73	            self.conn.commit()
    74	
    75	    def save_order(self, data):
    76	        oid = str(data.get('orderId')) if data.get('orderId') is not None else None
    77	        vals = (
    78	            data.get('side'), data.get('type'), str(data.get('orderListId')) if data.get('orderListId') is not None else None,
    79	            data.get('clientOrderId'), data.get('status'), self._num(data.get('price')), self._num(data.get('stopPrice')),
    80	            self._num(data.get('origQty')), json.dumps(data, default=str)
    81	        )
    82	        if oid:
    83	            r = self.conn.execute('SELECT id FROM orders WHERE symbol=? AND order_id=?', (data.get('symbol'), oid)).fetchone()
    84	            if r:
    85	                self.conn.execute('UPDATE orders SET side=?,type=?,order_list_id=?,client_order_id=?,status=?,price=?,stop_price=?,quantity=?,raw_json=? WHERE id=?', vals + (r['id'],))
    86	                self.conn.commit(); return
    87	        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO orders(symbol,side,type,order_id,order_list_id,client_order_id,status,price,stop_price,quantity,raw_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', (
    88	            data.get('symbol'), data.get('side'), data.get('type'), oid, str(data.get('orderListId')) if data.get('orderListId') is not None else None,
    89	            data.get('clientOrderId'), data.get('status'), self._num(data.get('price')), self._num(data.get('stopPrice')), self._num(data.get('origQty')), json.dumps(data, default=str)))
    90	        self.conn.commit()
    91	
    92	    @staticmethod
    93	    def _num(v):
    94	        if v in (None, ''): return None
    95	        try: return float(v)
    96	        except (TypeError, ValueError): return None
    97	
    98	    def open_trade(self, symbol=None):
    99	        if symbol:
   100	            r = self.conn.execute(
   101	                'SELECT * FROM trades WHERE symbol=? AND exit_time IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1',
   102	                (str(symbol).upper(),)
   103	            ).fetchone()
   104	        else:
   105	            r = self.conn.execute(
   106	                'SELECT * FROM trades WHERE exit_time IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1'
   107	            ).fetchone()
   108	        return dict(r) if r else None
   109	
   110	    def save_trade(self, **kwargs):
   111	        cols = ','.join(kwargs)
   112	        cur = self.conn.execute(
   113	            f'INSERT INTO trades({cols}) VALUES({",".join("?" for _ in kwargs)})',
   114	            tuple(kwargs.values())
   115	        )
   116	        self.conn.commit()
   117	        return cur.lastrowid
   118	
   119	    def update_trade_oco(self, trade_id, order_list_id):
   120	        self.conn.execute('UPDATE trades SET exit_order_list_id=? WHERE id=? AND exit_time IS NULL', (str(order_list_id), trade_id))
   121	        self.conn.commit()
   122	
   123	    def close_trade(self, trade_id, exit_time, exit_price, pnl, pnl_pct, reason, exit_order_list_id=None, fees=0):
   124	        self.conn.execute('UPDATE trades SET exit_time=?,exit_price=?,pnl=?,pnl_pct=?,reason=?,exit_order_list_id=COALESCE(?,exit_order_list_id),fees=? WHERE id=? AND exit_time IS NULL', (exit_time, exit_price, pnl, pnl_pct, reason, exit_order_list_id, fees, trade_id))
   125	        self.conn.commit()
   126	
   127	    def recent_orders(self, symbol, limit=100):
   128	        return [dict(r) for r in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM orders WHERE symbol=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?', (symbol, limit)).fetchall()]
   129	
   130	    def trades_today(self, symbol):
   131	        return int(self.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trades WHERE symbol=? AND entry_time >= date('now')", (symbol,)).fetchone()[0])
   132	
   133	    def pnl_today(self, symbol):
   134	        r = self.conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pnl),0) AS pnl FROM trades WHERE symbol=? AND exit_time IS NOT NULL AND exit_time >= date('now')", (symbol,)).fetchone()
   135	        return float(r['pnl'] or 0)
   136	
   137	    def consecutive_losses(self, symbol, limit=20):
   138	        rows = self.conn.execute('SELECT pnl FROM trades WHERE symbol=? AND exit_time IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?', (symbol, limit)).fetchall()
   139	        n = 0
   140	        for r in rows:
   141	            if float(r['pnl'] or 0) < 0: n += 1
   142	            else: break
   143	        return n
   144	
   145	    def last_exit_time(self, symbol):
   146	        r = self.conn.execute('SELECT exit_time FROM trades WHERE symbol=? AND exit_time IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1', (symbol,)).fetchone()
   147	        return r['exit_time'] if r else None

############################
# 25. CREDENTIAL STORE
############################
     1	import base64
     2	import json
     3	import os
     4	from pathlib import Path
     5	from threading import RLock
     6	
     7	from cryptography.fernet import Fernet, InvalidToken
     8	
     9	
    10	class CredentialStore:
    11	    """Small encrypted-at-rest store for Binance credentials.
    12	
    13	    The encryption key is preferably supplied by CREDENTIALS_ENCRYPTION_KEY.
    14	    For a self-contained VPS install, if it is absent a random key is generated
    15	    in data/credential_master.key with restrictive permissions and reused after restart.
    16	    """
    17	
    18	    def __init__(self, path="data/binance_credentials.enc", key_path="data/credential_master.key"):
    19	        self.path = Path(path)
    20	        self.key_path = Path(key_path)
    21	        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    22	        self.key_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    23	        self._lock = RLock()
    24	        self._fernet = Fernet(self._load_key())
    25	
    26	    def _load_key(self) -> bytes:
    27	        raw = os.getenv("CREDENTIALS_ENCRYPTION_KEY", "").strip()
    28	        if raw:
    29	            try:
    30	                Fernet(raw.encode())
    31	                return raw.encode()
    32	            except Exception as exc:
    33	                raise RuntimeError("CREDENTIALS_ENCRYPTION_KEY is invalid.") from exc
    34	
    35	        if self.key_path.exists():
    36	            key = self.key_path.read_bytes().strip()
    37	            try:
    38	                Fernet(key)
    39	                return key
    40	            except Exception as exc:
    41	                raise RuntimeError("Stored credential encryption key is invalid.") from exc
    42	
    43	        key = Fernet.generate_key()
    44	        self.key_path.write_bytes(key + b"\n")
    45	        try:
    46	            os.chmod(self.key_path, 0o600)
    47	        except OSError:
    48	            pass
    49	        return key
    50	
    51	    def save(self, api_key: str, api_secret: str, testnet: bool) -> None:
    52	        payload = json.dumps({
    53	            "api_key": api_key,
    54	            "api_secret": api_secret,
    55	            "testnet": bool(testnet),
    56	        }, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
    57	        encrypted = self._fernet.encrypt(payload)
    58	        tmp = self.path.with_suffix(self.path.suffix + ".tmp")
    59	        with self._lock:
    60	            tmp.write_bytes(encrypted + b"\n")
    61	            try:
    62	                os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
    63	            except OSError:
    64	                pass
    65	            tmp.replace(self.path)
    66	            try:
    67	                os.chmod(self.path, 0o600)
    68	            except OSError:
    69	                pass
    70	
    71	    def load(self):
    72	        with self._lock:
    73	            if not self.path.exists():
    74	                return None
    75	            try:
    76	                payload = self._fernet.decrypt(self.path.read_bytes().strip())
    77	                data = json.loads(payload.decode())
    78	                if not data.get("api_key") or not data.get("api_secret"):
    79	                    return None
    80	                return {
    81	                    "api_key": str(data["api_key"]),
    82	                    "api_secret": str(data["api_secret"]),
    83	                    "testnet": bool(data.get("testnet", True)),
    84	                }
    85	            except (InvalidToken, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    86	                raise RuntimeError("Encrypted Binance credentials cannot be decrypted.") from exc
    87	
    88	    def clear(self) -> None:
    89	        with self._lock:
    90	            try:
    91	                self.path.unlink()
    92	            except FileNotFoundError:
    93	                pass

----- app/build.gradle.kts -----
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.util.Properties
import java.util.Base64

plugins {
    id("com.android.application")
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android")
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose")
}

val keystorePropertiesFile = rootProject.file("keystore.properties")
val keystoreProperties = Properties()
if (keystorePropertiesFile.exists()) {
    FileInputStream(keystorePropertiesFile).use { keystoreProperties.load(it) }
}

val ciRunNumber = System.getenv("GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER")?.toIntOrNull()
val resolvedVersionCode = maxOf(10, ciRunNumber ?: 10)

val debugKeystoreFile = rootProject.file("build/williams-debug.p12")
val debugKeystorePassword = System.getenv("WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD")
val debugKeystoreBase64 = System.getenv("WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64")

if (!debugKeystoreBase64.isNullOrBlank() && !debugKeystorePassword.isNullOrBlank()) {
    debugKeystoreFile.parentFile.mkdirs()
    if (!debugKeystoreFile.exists()) {
        Base64.getDecoder().decode(debugKeystoreBase64).let {
            debugKeystoreFile.writeBytes(it)
        }
    }
}

android {
    namespace = "com.williamsbot"
    compileSdk = 35

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.williamsbot"
        minSdk = 26
        targetSdk = 35
        versionCode = resolvedVersionCode
        versionName = "4.11.0"
    }

    buildFeatures {
        compose = true
    }

    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }

    kotlinOptions {
        jvmTarget = "17"
    }

    buildTypes {
        debug {
            applicationIdSuffix = ".debug"
            versionNameSuffix = "-debug"
            isDebuggable = true

            if (debugKeystoreFile.exists() && !debugKeystorePassword.isNullOrBlank()) {
                signingConfig = signingConfigs.create("williamsDebug") {
                    storeFile = debugKeystoreFile
                    storePassword = debugKeystorePassword
                    storeType = "PKCS12"
                    keyAlias = "williams-debug"
                    keyPassword = debugKeystorePassword
                }
            }
        }

        release {
            isMinifyEnabled = false
            isShrinkResources = false
            isDebuggable = false
            proguardFiles(
                getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"),
                "proguard-rules.pro"
            )

            if (keystorePropertiesFile.exists()) {
                signingConfig = signingConfigs.create("release") {
                    storeFile = rootProject.file(
                        keystoreProperties.getProperty("storeFile")
                    )
                    storePassword = keystoreProperties.getProperty("storePassword")
                    keyAlias = keystoreProperties.getProperty("keyAlias")
                    keyPassword = keystoreProperties.getProperty("keyPassword")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    packaging {
        resources {
            excludes += "/META-INF/{AL2.0,LGPL2.1}"
        }
    }
}

dependencies {
    implementation("androidx.core:core-ktx:1.15.0")
    implementation("androidx.activity:activity-compose:1.10.0")
    implementation(platform("androidx.compose:compose-bom:2025.01.00"))
    implementation("androidx.compose.ui:ui")
    implementation("androidx.compose.ui:ui-tooling-preview")
    implementation("androidx.compose.material3:material3")
    implementation("androidx.compose.material:material-icons-extended")
    implementation("androidx.lifecycle:lifecycle-runtime-compose:2.8.7")
    implementation("com.squareup.okhttp3:okhttp:4.12.0")
    implementation("androidx.security:security-crypto:1.1.0-alpha06")
    implementation("com.google.android.gms:play-services-code-scanner:16.1.0")
    debugImplementation("androidx.compose.ui:ui-tooling")
}

# 30. GITHUB ACTIONS

.github/workflows/android-apk.yml


----- .github/workflows/android-apk.yml -----
name: Build Android APK

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master]
    paths:
      - 'app/**'
      - '*.gradle.kts'
      - 'gradle.properties'
      - 'gradle/**'
      - 'settings.gradle.kts'
      - '.github/workflows/**'
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - 'app/**'
      - '*.gradle.kts'
      - 'gradle.properties'
      - 'gradle/**'
      - 'settings.gradle.kts'
      - '.github/workflows/**'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: android-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

env:
  GRADLE_VERSION: '8.9'
  ANDROID_PLATFORM: 'platforms;android-35'
  ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS: 'build-tools;35.0.0'

jobs:
  build-apk:
    name: Build APKs
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v5
        with:
          fetch-depth: 1

      - name: Set up JDK 17
        uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'
          cache: gradle

      - name: Set up Android SDK
        uses: android-actions/setup-android@v4

      - name: Install Android SDK packages
        run: |
          yes | sdkmanager --licenses >/dev/null || true
          sdkmanager "${ANDROID_PLATFORM}" "${ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS}"

      - name: Set up Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v5
        with:
          gradle-version: ${{ env.GRADLE_VERSION }}
          validate-wrappers: false

      - name: Restore persistent debug signing key
        env:
          DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64 }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          if [ -z "${DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64:-}" ]; then
            echo "::error::WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64 GitHub Secret is not configured."
            echo "The debug APK must use a persistent signing key."
            exit 1
          fi

          mkdir -p build

          printf '%s' "$DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64" \
            | base64 --decode > build/williams-debug.p12

          chmod 600 build/williams-debug.p12

          echo "Persistent debug keystore restored."

      - name: Validate debug signing configuration
        env:
          WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64 }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          test -s build/williams-debug.p12

          echo "Debug signing material: PRESENT"

      - name: Build debug APK
        env:
          WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_B64 }}
        run: |
          gradle --no-daemon :app:assembleDebug --stacktrace

      - name: Build release APK
        run: |
          gradle --no-daemon :app:assembleRelease --stacktrace

      - name: Verify APK files
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          DEBUG_APK="app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk"
          RELEASE_APK="app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk"

          test -f "$DEBUG_APK"
          test -f "$RELEASE_APK"

          echo "=== APK files ==="
          ls -lh "$DEBUG_APK" "$RELEASE_APK"

      - name: Inspect APK metadata
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo "=== DEBUG APK ==="
          "$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/35.0.0/aapt" dump badging \
            app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk \
            | grep -E "package:|sdkVersion:|targetSdkVersion:"

          echo
          echo "=== RELEASE APK ==="
          "$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/35.0.0/aapt" dump badging \
            app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk \
            | grep -E "package:|sdkVersion:|targetSdkVersion:"

      - name: Verify persistent debug keystore
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          test -s build/williams-debug.p12
          echo "Persistent debug PKCS12 keystore: PRESENT"

      - name: Verify persistent debug APK signature
        env:
          WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          APK="app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk"
          APKSIGNER="$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/35.0.0/apksigner"
          KEYTOOL="$JAVA_HOME/bin/keytool"

          test -f "$APK"
          test -f "$APKSIGNER"
          test -f "build/williams-debug.p12"

          KEYSTORE_SHA="$("$KEYTOOL" -list -v             -keystore build/williams-debug.p12             -storetype PKCS12             -storepass "$WILLIAMS_DEBUG_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD"             -alias williams-debug             | awk -F': ' '/SHA256:/{print $2; exit}')"

          APK_SHA="$("$APKSIGNER" verify --print-certs "$APK"             | awk -F': ' '/Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest:/{print $2; exit}')"

          echo "Keystore SHA-256: $KEYSTORE_SHA"
          echo "APK SHA-256:      $APK_SHA"

          test -n "$KEYSTORE_SHA"
          test -n "$APK_SHA"

          KEYSTORE_SHA_NORMALIZED="$(printf '%s' "$KEYSTORE_SHA" | tr -d ':' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
          APK_SHA_NORMALIZED="$(printf '%s' "$APK_SHA" | tr -d ':' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"

          test "$KEYSTORE_SHA_NORMALIZED" = "$APK_SHA_NORMALIZED"

          echo "Signing certificate match: PASS"

          "$APKSIGNER" verify --verbose "$APK"

          echo "Persistent debug signing verification: PASS"

      - name: Generate SHA-256 checksums
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          sha256sum \
            app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk \
            > app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk.sha256

          sha256sum \
            app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk \
            > app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk.sha256

          echo "=== SHA-256 ==="
          cat app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk.sha256
          cat app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk.sha256

      - name: Upload installable debug APK
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: williams-bot-debug-apk-${{ github.run_number }}
          path: |
            app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
            app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk.sha256
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 14

      - name: Upload release APK
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: williams-bot-release-unsigned-${{ github.run_number }}
          path: |
            app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk
            app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk.sha256
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 14

      - name: Build summary
        run: |
          {
            echo '## Android build complete'
            echo ''
            echo "Version code: ${{ github.run_number }}"
            echo ''
            echo '### APKs'
            echo '- Installable debug APK: `app-debug.apk`'
            echo '- Release APK: `app-release-unsigned.apk`'
            echo ''
            echo '### Signing'
            echo '- Debug: persistent GitHub Secret keystore'
            echo '- Release: existing release signing configuration when available'
          } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
